### [1] [TinyJudge: Unverifiable Constraint Alignment via Lightweight Specialist Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07520)
*Yirong Zeng,Yufei Liu,Xiao Ding,Yutai Hou,Yuxian Wang,Wu Ning,Haonan Song,Dandan Tu,Qixun Zhang,Yuxiang He,Bibo Cai,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TinyJudge框架，使用小型语言模型（~0.6B）集成来为不可验证约束提供奖励，解决RLHF中奖励黑客和计算开销高的问题，在IF任务上提升~10%性能且实现3倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL+可验证奖励方法在处理不可验证约束（如语气）时存在严重的奖励黑客问题和较高的计算开销，需要更高效、鲁棒的评估方案。

Method: 提出TinyJudge框架：1）分析不可验证约束的泛化能力，发现特定约束具有高泛化模式；2）采用多个专用小型语言模型（~0.6B）集成，从前沿模型蒸馏专业知识，实现轻量级高精度奖励评估。

Result: 在五个基准上，TinyJudge相比基线平均性能提升~10%，奖励精度提升12%，同时训练总时间实现3倍加速。

Conclusion: TinyJudge通过小型模型集成蒸馏为LLM对齐不可验证指令提供了可扩展、鲁棒的路径，在效率和效果上均有显著优势。

Abstract: Instruction Following (IF) is a core capability of LLMs, requiring strict adherence to diverse constraints, ranging from verifiable ones (e.g., output length) to unverifiable ones (e.g., tone). Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has emerged as a paradigm for IF tasks, leveraging LLM-as-a-judge to assess unverifiable constraints. However, we empirically find that this approach remains a significant bottleneck, suffering from severe reward hacking and higher computational overhead. In this work, we first analyze the generalization capabilities of unverifiable constraints and discover that specific constraints exhibit distinct, high-generalization patterns. Motivated by this, we propose TinyJudge, a framework that employs an ensemble of specialized tiny language models ($\sim0.6B$) to provide rewards for soft constraints. By distilling expertise from frontier models into these tiny models, it achieves high-precision, lightweight evaluation. Extensive evaluations across five benchmarks demonstrate that TinyJudge outperforms the baselines by $\sim10\%$ in average performance and $12\%$ in reward precision. Crucially, it also achieves a $3\times$ speedup in total training time. Our work provides a scalable and robust path for aligning LLMs with unverifiable human instructions.

</details>


### [2] [AlignFed: Alignment-Aware Asynchronous Federated Fine-Tuning for Large Language Models in Heterogeneous Edge Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08197)
*Yan Wang,Ziyi Gao,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AlignFed，一种异步联邦微调框架，通过多阶段语义对齐机制（版本感知更新分组、跨版本语义对齐、公平性感知聚合）解决异构边缘环境中LLM联邦微调面临的模型漂移、客户端漂移和聚合不公平问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上LLM的协作适配面临数据隐私约束、计算与通信资源高度异构、本地数据非独立同分布等严峻挑战。传统的同步联邦聚合存在严重的掉队者效应（straggler effect），导致高系统延迟和低资源利用率，而现有异步联邦学习方法主要针对中小规模模型设计，难以应对LLM微调中因陈旧更新导致的模型漂移、数据异构加剧的客户端漂移以及快速客户端主导造成的聚合不公平。

Method: 提出AlignFed框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 版本感知更新分组，将更新按版本分组管理；(2) 基于小批量校准集的跨版本语义对齐，缓解不同版本间的语义偏移；(3) 公平性感知聚合，综合考虑更新新鲜度和客户端参与频率，实现更公平的聚合权重分配。

Result: 在高度异构和存在显著更新陈旧性的场景下，AlignFed能有效缓解跨版本模型漂移和客户端漂移，提升聚合公平性，实现稳定高效的异步联邦优化。

Conclusion: AlignFed为异构边缘环境中LLM的异步联邦微调提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过语义对齐和公平性感知聚合克服了传统方法的关键局限。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly propelled the advancement of edge intelligence and have been widely deployed across various scenarios, including autonomous driving, industrial inspection, and personalized IoT services. However, the collaborative adaptation of LLMs on edge devices continues to face formidable challenges due to strict data privacy constraints, highly heterogeneous computing and communication resources, and the non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) nature of local data. Federated Fine-Tuning (FFT) enables the collaborative optimization of distributed models without exposing raw data. Yet, traditional synchronous aggregation suffers from a severe straggler effect, resulting in high system latency and low resource utilization. Existing asynchronous federated learning methods are predominantly designed for small-to-medium-scale models and struggle to address the specific challenges inherent in LLM fine-tuning namely, model drift caused by stale updates, aggravated client drift stemming from data heterogeneity, and aggregation fairness imbalance resulting from the dominance of fast clients. To address these issues, this paper proposes AlignFed, an asynchronous federated fine-tuning framework for LLMs tailored to heterogeneous edge environments. AlignFed employs a lightweight multi-stage semantic alignment mechanism comprising three core modules: version-aware update grouping, cross-version semantic alignment based on a mini-batch calibration set, and fairness-aware aggregation that integrates both update freshness and client participation frequency. This framework effectively mitigates cross-version model drift and client drift while enhancing aggregation fairness, thereby achieving stable and efficient asynchronous federated optimization in scenarios characterized by high heterogeneity and significant update staleness.

</details>


### [3] [Subtitle-Aligned Fine-Tuning of Whisper for Swiss German ASR: Benchmark Contamination, Convention Mismatch, and an Honest Baseline at 25.6% WER (13.8% cWER)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07608)
*Felix Akeret*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在NVIDIA DGX Spark上对Whisper large-v3进行瑞士德语ASR的微调，通过1367小时弱监督数据和16轮迭代训练，最佳模型实测WER 25.6%，内容WER 13.8%。同时揭示了现有SOTA结果因基准污染而虚高，并发布了两个公开模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瑞士德语缺乏标注数据和专用ASR模型，现有方法在真实场景下表现不佳，且发现公开的SOTA结果存在基准污染问题。

Method: 使用1367小时广播语音配标准德语字幕作为弱监督，在DGX Spark上对Whisper large-v3进行16轮LoRA和全参数微调，并进行幻觉根因分析、数据质量影响量化等系统研究。

Result: 最佳模型实测WER 25.6%，cWER 13.8%；发现公开SOTA结果因基准污染被高估，原始Whisper零瑞士德语数据自训练即可达到13.88% WER。

Conclusion: 发布了两个公开可复现的瑞士德语Whisper模型，揭示了ASR基准测试中数据污染问题的严重性，并提出了更诚实的评估方法（cWER）。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of fine-tuning OpenAI's Whisper large-v3 for Swiss German ASR, using 1,367 hours of broadcast speech paired with Standard German subtitles as weak supervision. Through 16 iterative training runs on an NVIDIA DGX Spark (Grace Blackwell, 128 GB unified memory, up to 1 PFLOP FP4), we compare LoRA and full fine-tuning of the 1.55B-parameter model, investigate hallucination root causes, and quantify the effect of data quality, subtitle alignment, and training strategy. Our best model achieves 25.6% measured WER on the All Swiss German Dialects Test Set (ASGDTS) in an honest evaluation on strictly disjoint data. A harmonized error analysis separating genuine errors from valid stylistic variation (tense, word order, Swiss orthography) yields a content WER (cWER) of 13.8%, counting only actual recognition failures. Bias-corrected estimation reduces this to 8.5%, suggesting the true error rate is roughly one third of measured WER.
  We demonstrate that published state-of-the-art Swiss German ASR results (17.1-17.5% WER) are inflated by benchmark contamination: a vanilla Whisper model self-trained on the ASGDTS test set with zero Swiss German data achieves 13.88% WER, surpassing all published systems. Experiments with Phi-4-multimodal show an even stronger memorization effect (3.9% WER), revealing that the benchmark primarily measures convention matching rather than dialectal comprehension.
  We release two models, a LoRA adapter (25.32% WER, 13.9% cWER) and a full fine-tuned model (25.60% WER, 13.8% cWER), among the few publicly available, honestly evaluated Whisper models for Swiss German, under Apache 2.0 with full reproducibility, requiring no institutional data agreements.

</details>


### [4] [Unlocking Latent Value: Taxonomy-Guided Recovery of High-Performing Data from Low-Tier Web Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07778)
*Neeraj Varshney,Sanket Lokegaonkar,Nasser Zalmout,Qingyu Yin,Priyanka Nigam,Bing Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多维度分类法（ESSENTIAL-WEB）的Web数据过滤框架，引入时效性和文化特异性两个新维度，通过两阶段计算高效筛选策略，从被降级处理的Web数据中恢复高质量内容，在推理和编程基准上显著超越未过滤的高质量数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web数据预处理流水线将文档质量压缩为单一综合分数，系统性地遗漏了评分器权重不足的高价值内容维度，需要一种能沿语义上有意义的维度进行过滤的方法来恢复这些价值。

Method: 基于ESSENTIAL-WEB分类法框架，引入时效性和文化特异性两个新维度；使用Qwen2.5 32B标注1400万文档并蒸馏为0.5B轻量模型；训练73M多任务MLP实现50倍推理吞吐量；提出两阶段计算高效过滤框架：Pass 1在小规模识别最强维度信号，Pass 2构建并评估联合/析取复合过滤器。

Result: 在中档数据上，最佳过滤器在推理、编程和知识基准上分别比未过滤基线提升12.1%、9.5%和2.0%，且超过未过滤的高质量数据6.7%（推理）和13.7%（编程）。低于典型生产阈值两个层级的数据经过滤后，推理和编程分别提升22.3%和19.5%，在编程基准上超越高质量数据。

Conclusion: 被降级处理的Web数据中仍蕴藏着大量潜在价值，多维度分类法过滤是一种原则性强且计算高效的解锁这些价值的关键方法。

Abstract: Dominant web data curation pipelines for pretraining collapse document quality into a single composite score, systematically missing high-value content along dimensions the scorer underweights. We present a taxonomy-driven framework that recovers this value by filtering along semantically meaningful dimensions that composite scores fail to capture. First, building on the ESSENTIAL-WEB taxonomy, we introduce two novel dimensions: timeliness and cultural specificity, both of which show low pairwise NMI with existing ones. We annotate 14M documents using Qwen2.5 32B and distill into a lightweight 0.5B model. To enable rapid corpus-wide annotation, we additionally train a 73M multi-task MLP on E5 embeddings, achieving 50x inference throughput. Second, to navigate the combinatorial explosion of filter configurations, we introduce a compute-efficient two-pass framework: Pass 1 identifies the strongest dimension signals at small scale; Pass 2 constructs and evaluates conjunctive and disjunctive compound filters from the top performers - identifying high-performing configurations at a fraction of full scaling-law cost. Applying the selected filters to deprioritized web data, taxonomy-filtered subsets outperform their unfiltered baselines and even surpass the highest-quality tier. On mid-tier data, our best filter improves over its unfiltered baseline by 12.1% on reasoning, 9.5% on coding, and 2.0% on knowledge benchmarks, exceeding unfiltered top-tier data by 6.7% on reasoning and 13.7% on coding. Furthermore, filtered data from two tiers below the typical production threshold improves by 22.3% on reasoning and 19.5% on coding over its unfiltered baseline, surpassing top-tier data on coding benchmarks. These results establish that vast latent value remains locked in deprioritized web data, and that multi-dimensional taxonomy filtering is a principled, compute-efficient key to unlocking it.

</details>


### [5] [Bayesian-Agent: Posterior-Guided Skill Evolution for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08348)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Wenjie Zhang,Zhichao Shi,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Bayesian-Agent框架，将可重用的技能和SOP视为关于冻结模型在特定提示、上下文和环境下能否成功的假设，通过贝叶斯后验更新来引导技能优化，替代启发式反思和简单计数方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent依赖的外部推理条件（提示、工具、记忆、SOP、技能等）通常通过启发式反思或简单计数成功/失败案例来修改，缺乏可靠的信念估计，导致优化不校准。

Method: 将可重用技能和SOP建模为条件化的后验概率假设，记录已验证轨迹证据，维护每个技能的特征条件分类后验，通过后验状态映射出可检查的动作（补丁、拆分、压缩、淘汰、探索），并给模型提示添加可执行护栏和故障模式补丁。

Result: 在deepseek-v4-flash上，SOP-Bench从80%提升到95%，Lifelong AgentBench从90%提升到100%，RealFin-Bench从45%提升到65%。同时评估了原生后端和可选的GenericAgent、mini-swe-agent、Claude Code后端。

Conclusion: Agent技能演化应被视为后验引导的harness优化，而非未校准的提示累积。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external inference conditions: prompts, tools, memory, SOPs, skills, and harness feedback. These assets can improve task execution without changing model weights, but they are often revised by heuristic reflection or by reusing observed successes and failures as if counts alone were reliable belief. We introduce \textbf{Bayesian-Agent}, a native and cross-harness framework that treats reusable skills and SOPs as hypotheses about whether a frozen model will succeed under a particular prompt, context, and harness environment. Bayesian-Agent records verified trajectory evidence, maintains a feature-conditioned categorical posterior over each skill, and maps posterior state into inspectable actions such as patch, split, compress, retire, and explore. Model-facing prompts receive executable guardrails and failure-mode patches, while posterior summaries remain available for audit. With \texttt{deepseek-v4-flash}, incremental repair improves SOP-Bench from 80\% to 95\%, Lifelong AgentBench from 90\% to 100\%, and RealFin-Bench from 45\% to 65\%. We further evaluate Bayesian-Agent's native backend and optional GenericAgent, mini-swe-agent, and Claude Code backends. The results include positive, negative, saturated, and case-study settings, suggesting that agent skill evolution is best viewed as posterior-guided harness optimization rather than uncalibrated prompt accumulation. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/Bayesian-Agent.

</details>


### [6] [AsyncLane: Decoupling Refinement from Advancement in Diffusion Language Model Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08411)
*Yingxuan Ren,Yuxuan Lou,Yong Liu,Pengcheng Fang,Ziming Wang,Pengfei Zhou,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AsyncLane 是一种无需训练的扩散大模型解码调度器，通过将生成行分叉为精炼行和延续行，解耦块内精炼与块间推进，利用共享前缀批处理、lookahead草稿复用、级联终止和紧凑缓存刷新等技术，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（DLM）的块级半自回归解码存在严格的块间依赖：当前块完全解码或去噪预算耗尽后下一块才能开始。然而一旦块暴露出可靠的分隔边界或稳定的语义前缀，延续生成无需等待每个残差token都解析完毕，存在并行化机会。

Method: AsyncLane 在检测到分隔边界时，将生成行（lane）分叉为精炼行（refine lane）和延续行（continuation generate lane）：前缀保持可编辑状态，延续行在前缀精炼完成前即开始推进。利用行树（lane tree）记录依赖和输出顺序，执行在活跃行集上进行。结合共享前缀行批处理、lookahead草稿复用、级联终止和紧凑缓存刷新与刷新logit复用，防止模型调用成本随行数线性增长。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，AsyncLane 在所有评估的基准长度设置中均实现最高TPS；相比最快的竞争基线，在LLaDA上达2.95倍加速，在Dream上达3.04倍加速，较长生成预算下优势更明显。

Conclusion: AsyncLane 可作为块级DLM采样器的即插即用替代方案，无需重新训练，在显著提升吞吐量的同时保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Abstract: Block-wise semi-autoregressive decoding is the standard inference paradigm for diffusion large language models (DLMs), but it imposes a strict dependency between blocks: the next block cannot begin until the current block is fully decoded or its denoising budget is exhausted. We observe that once a block exposes a reliable delimiter boundary or stable semantic prefix, continuation generation need not wait for every residual token to be resolved. We propose AsyncLane, a training-free decoding scheduler that decouples refinement from advancement. AsyncLane forks a generate lane at observed delimiter boundaries into a refine lane and a continuation generate lane: the prefix remains editable, while the continuation advances before prefix refinement finishes. The resulting lane tree records decoding dependencies and output order, while execution proceeds over the active lane set. To make this asynchronous schedule efficient under bidirectional attention, AsyncLane combines shared-prefix lane batching, lookahead draft reuse, cascading termination, and compact cache refresh with refresh-logit reuse, preventing model-call cost from scaling directly with the number of lanes. AsyncLane is a drop-in replacement for block-wise DLM samplers and requires no retraining. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation show that AsyncLane consistently improves throughput while maintaining competitive quality. Across LLaDA and Dream backbones, AsyncLane achieves the highest TPS in all evaluated benchmark-length settings; relative to the fastest competing baseline, it reaches peak speedups of 2.95x on LLaDA and 3.04x on Dream, with especially large gains under longer generation budgets.

</details>


### [7] [Calibration of Structured Ignorance Certificates for Diagnosing Unknown Unknowns in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08571)
*Subramanyam Sahoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出结构化无知证书（SICs），通过JSON格式输出让模型主动承认知识边界、命名缺失领域、列出所需概念并提出检索查询，而非产生幻觉。使用GRPO微调14B模型，在跨领域未知问题上达到99.46% JSON有效性和0.967证书特异性分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常以流畅但错误的回答掩盖无知，需要一种机制让模型主动承认知识边界并引导正确检索，而非产生幻觉。

Method: 构建7,347样本的Unknown-Unknown数据集（跨领域组合问题），使用GRPO微调14B模型，奖励函数结合检索效用、概念特异性和格式有效性，训练模型输出SICs。

Result: SIC模型达到99.46% JSON有效性和0.967证书特异性分数，在检索接地生成上比基座模型提升3.6% ROUGE-L。

Conclusion: 显式的认知结构建模（让模型承认无知并提出检索路径）是可学习且可衡量的能力，能有效减少幻觉。

Abstract: Large language models frequently fail in a characteristic way: rather than acknowledging ignorance, they produce fluent but incorrect answers to questions that lie beyond their knowledge boundaries. We introduce \textbf{Structured Ignorance Certificates} (SICs), a JSON-formatted output schema that demands a model explicitly name the missing domain intersection, enumerate required concepts, and propose a productive retrieval query rather than hallucinating an answer. To train models to produce high-quality SICs we construct a 7,347-sample \emph{Unknown-Unknown} (UU) dataset by prompting Qwen3-14B to stitch together questions from seven domains (physics, biology, engineering, CS, economics, medical, legal) into novel cross-domain queries that no single-domain expert could answer. We fine-tune a 14B-parameter model with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) using a composite reward that combines retrieval utility, concept specificity, and output-format validity. A paraphrase-divergence probe trained on model responses confirms that SIC-tuned outputs systematically exhibit higher unknown-unknown probability scores. Evaluation on 735 held-out UU questions achieves a 99.46\% JSON validity rate, a mean Certificate Specificity Score of 0.967, and a 3.6\% ROUGE-L improvement over the base model on retrieval-grounded generation -- demonstrating that explicit epistemic structuring is a learnable and measurable capability.

</details>


### [8] [HydraQE: OSU's Submission for the IWSLT 2026 Speech Translation Metrics Shared Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08748)
*Kevin Krahn,Eric Fosler-Lussier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HydraQE 是一个端到端的语音翻译质量估计系统，基于 Qwen3-ASR 骨干网络，通过可学习的 sparsemax 标量混合、轻量双向 Transformer 跨模态交互和多头预测（DA、MetricX-24、xCOMET），在合成数据、伪标签数据和人标注数据上采用课程学习训练，在 IWSLT 2026 评测中优于级联基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音翻译质量估计（QE）传统上依赖级联的 ASR + 文本 QE 方法，缺乏端到端的直接评估方案，且人工标注数据稀缺。

Method: 基于 Qwen3-ASR 骨干网络，将源音频和翻译假设联合输入，使用可学习 sparsemax 标量混合所有层的隐藏状态，再经轻量双向 Transformer 进行跨模态交互，最后池化为共享嵌入。三个独立预测头分别训练于 DA 标注、MetricX-24 伪标签和 xCOMET 伪标签。采用课程学习：先训练合成数据和伪标签数据，再逐步过渡到人工标注数据。

Result: HydraQE 在 IWSLT 2026 语音翻译度量共享任务中优于级联的基于文本的基线方法和先前的直接语音 QE 系统。

Conclusion: 端到端的语音翻译质量估计方法可以与级联方法竞争，为语音翻译评估提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: We present HydraQE, our contribution to the IWSLT 2026 Speech Translation Metrics shared task. HydraQE is an end-to-end, reference-free quality estimation (QE) system for speech translation built on a Qwen3-ASR backbone, which accepts source audio and a translation hypothesis as joint input. Hidden states from all backbone layers are combined via a learnable sparsemax scalar mix, then re-encoded by a lightweight bidirectional Transformer to enable full cross-modal interaction prior to pooling into a shared embedding. Three independent prediction heads are trained on complementary supervision signals: human direct assessment (DA) annotations, MetricX-24 pseudo-labels, and xCOMET pseudo-labels. To address the scarcity of human-annotated data, we train on a combination of synthetically corrupted examples and silver pseudo-labeled machine translation outputs, using a curriculum that begins on synthetic and silver data and gradually shifts toward human-annotated examples. HydraQE outperforms cascaded text-based baselines and prior direct speech QE systems, demonstrating that end-to-end speech translation QE is competitive with cascaded approaches.

</details>


### [9] [PerspectiveGap: A Benchmark for Multi-Agent Orchestration Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08878)
*Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Kejia Zhang,Xinpeng Liu,Jiaxuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出PerspectiveGap基准测试，用于评估LLM在multi-agent系统中编写编排提示的能力。包含110个场景、10种拓扑结构，测试了27个商业模型。结果显示GPT-5.5最佳但平均通过率仅14.9%，表明多智能体编排提示是一项独特且未被充分评估的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM应用正从单智能体向多智能体系统演进，但现有模型难以确定每个子智能体需要知道什么信息，缺乏评估多智能体编排提示能力的基准。

Method: 构建PerspectiveGap基准，包含110个源自实际工程实践的场景，组织为10种拓扑结构，采用角色-片段分配和自由文本提示两种任务格式，并引入Prompt Economy原则（构建以循环为中心的编排，最大化效用同时最小化角色和工程开销）。

Result: 测试27个商业模型，GPT-5.5显著优于其他模型，Opus 4.7在编排提示方面表现不佳。模型平均通过率仅14.9%（GPT-5.5 62.0%），平均泄漏率246.5%（GPT-5.5 49.1%）。

Conclusion: 多智能体编排提示是一项独特且未被充分评估的能力，PerspectiveGap为系统化衡量和提升该能力提供了基础。

Abstract: Real-world LLM applications are moving beyond single-agent workflows toward orchestrated multi-agent systems, yet current models still struggle to determine what each sub-agent needs to know. To measure this, we introduce PerspectiveGap, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs' ability to compose orchestration prompts for multi-agent systems. PerspectiveGap contains 110 scenarios, each evaluated through two distractor-mixed task formats: role-fragment assignment and free-form prompt writing. These scenarios are organized into 10 topologies, which are distilled from the authors' real-world engineering practice and framed by the Prompt Economy principle: building loop-centered orchestrations that maximize utility with minimal role and engineering overhead. In experiments with 27 commercial models from 10 companies, GPT-5.5 substantially outperforms all competitors, whereas Opus 4.7 shows a notable weakness in orchestration prompting despite its strong coding performance. Nevertheless, PerspectiveGap remains challenging: the evaluated models achieve an average combined pass rate of only 14.9\% (GPT-5.5 62.0\%) and an average overall leakage rate of 246.5\% (a per-scenario information leak-event count, not a proportion; GPT-5.5 49.1\%). These findings suggest that multi-agent orchestration prompting is a distinct and under-evaluated capability, and PerspectiveGap provides a foundation for measuring and improving it systematically.

</details>


### [10] [SafeRun: Enabling Determinism in LLM Planning for Running](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09027)
*Meilin Chen,Zepeng Zhai,Jiaxuan Zhao,Yuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SafeRun是一个解耦架构框架，通过将LLM的软解释与确定性求解器的硬约束分离，实现LLM在跑步规划中的确定性安全规划，在五个LLM上达到100%安全率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在需要确定性的关键领域（如跑步规划中的安全约束）不可靠，其概率特性可能导致违反安全规则，存在安全风险。

Method: 提出解耦架构SafeRun，将LLM的语义解释（软）与确定性求解器的硬约束执行分离，既保证严格安全约束又保留自然语言灵活性。

Result: 在五个LLM上实验，SafeRun达到100%安全率（对比PE平均79.1%、CodeAct平均97.6%），同时保持有竞争力的指令遵循分数。

Conclusion: 解耦架构能有效解决LLM在确定性关键领域的规划安全问题，兼顾安全性与灵活性。

Abstract: Large Language Models enable flexible natural-language planning but remain unreliable in determinism-critical domains due to their probabilistic nature. This limitation is especially problematic in running planning, where violating safety rules can lead to safety risks. We propose SafeRun, a framework for deterministic LLM-based planning via a decoupled architecture. SafeRun separates soft interpretation by an LLM from hard constraint enforcement by a deterministic solver, ensuring strict safety constraints while preserving natural-language flexibility. To validate SafeRun, we build a comprehensive benchmark for running planning under realistic physiological and safety constraints. Experiments across five LLMs show that SafeRun achieves 100\% safety score (vs.\ 79.1\% PE average and 97.6\% CodeAct average) while maintaining competitive instruction-following scores. The SafeRun benchmark is publicly available at \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/zzp-seeker/SafeRun-RunPlanning-Benchmark}{huggingface}.

</details>


### [11] [What Should a Skill Remember? Quality-Cost Trade-offs in Cost-Aware Skill Rewriting for Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09421)
*Qinghua Xing,Yinda Chen,Yaping Jin,Zhenhe Wu,Bohan Lin,Hang Zhou,Xinghao Chen,Hanting Chen,Zhiwei Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将技能重写视为成本感知的操作知识工程而非简单的提示压缩，通过信息保留策略在技能质量与成本之间取得平衡，在SkillsBench上实验显示策略可降低7.0%总成本和6.0%下游token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将技能重写简单视为提示压缩，但更短的技能可能因移除稀疏操作锚点而导致Agent探索、调试和恢复成本增加，缺乏从经济角度系统研究技能重写的方法。

Method: 提出SkillEE框架：构建受控框架分析技能结构，使用信息保留策略（API/代码锚定、工作流守护、规则/公式锚定）重写技能，在固定任务指令、环境和验证器下评估重写效果。

Result: 在SkillsBench上，不同策略对不同任务族有不同质量-成本权衡；学习策略在held-out评估中降低7.0%总成本和6.0%下游token成本；跨模型迁移中平均降低14.7%和13.7%成本，且验证器质量保持不变。

Conclusion: 技能设计应被定位为成本感知的操作知识工程而非提示压缩，不同任务族需要不同的锚定策略。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on skills: reusable procedural documents encoding workflows, tool use, implementation patterns, validation checks, and domain rules. Skill rewriting is often treated as prompt compression, but shorter skills can make agents more expensive by removing sparse operational anchors that prevent exploration, debugging, and recovery. We study skill rewriting through this economic lens. Our controlled framework profiles skill structure, rewrites skills using information-preservation strategies, and evaluates the rewrites under fixed task instructions, environments, and verifiers. Experiments on SkillsBench reveal distinct quality--cost trade-offs across strategies: API/code anchoring, workflow guarding, and rule/formula anchoring benefit different task families, with no universally dominant template. In the main held-out evaluation, the learned policy reduces total cost by 7.0\% and downstream agent-token cost by 6.0\%; in frozen cross-model transfer, the corresponding reductions average 14.7\% and 13.7\%, while verifier quality is preserved. These results position skill design as cost-aware operational knowledge engineering rather than prompt compression. Resources: \href{https://github.com/1Reminding/Skill_EE}{SkillEE}.

</details>


### [12] [Self-Harness: Harnesses That Improve Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09498)
*Hangfan Zhang,Shao Zhang,Kangcong Li,Chen Zhang,Yang Chen,Yiqun Zhang,Lei Bai,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Harness范式，让LLM智能体无需人类专家或更强外部智能体，通过迭代循环（弱点挖掘、方案生成、方案验证）自动改进自身操作框架，在Terminal-Bench-2.0上使多个基础模型的通过率提升15-21个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的性能受基础模型和操作框架（harness）共同影响，但有效的框架设计具有模型特异性，当前依赖人类专家设计的范式难以扩展到日益多样化和快速演进的现代LLM。

Method: Self-Harness采用三阶段迭代循环：1) Weakness Mining：从执行轨迹中识别模型特定的失败模式；2) Harness Proposal：针对这些失败生成多样性且最小化的框架修改；3) Proposal Validation：通过回归测试接受候选修改。在Terminal-Bench-2.0上用最小初始框架和三个不同系列的基础模型进行实例化。

Result: 在MiniMax M2.5、Qwen3.5-35B-A3B、GLM-5三个模型上，保留集通过率分别从40.5%提升至61.9%、23.8%提升至38.1%、42.9%提升至57.1%。定性分析显示系统不会简单添加通用指令，而是将模型特定弱点转化为具体可执行的框架变更。

Conclusion: Self-Harness为LLM智能体提供了一条路径，使其不仅能被框架塑造，还能主动参与重塑自身框架，实现自我改进。

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by their base models and the harnesses that mediate their interaction with the environment. Because different models exhibit distinct behaviors, effective harness design is inherently model-specific. Yet agent harnesses are still largely engineered by human experts, a paradigm that scales poorly as modern LLMs become increasingly diverse and rapidly evolving. In this paper, we introduce Self-Harness, a new paradigm in which an LLM-based agent improves its own operating harness, without relying on human engineers or stronger external agents. We operationalize Self-Harness as an iterative loop with three stages: Weakness Mining, which identifies model-specific failure patterns from execution traces; Harness Proposal, which generates diverse yet minimal harness modifications tied to these failures; and Proposal Validation, which accepts candidate edits only after regression testing. We instantiate Self-Harness on Terminal-Bench-2.0 using a minimal initial harness and three base models from diverse families: MiniMax M2.5, Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, and GLM-5. Across all three models, Self-Harness consistently improves performance, with held-out pass rates increasing from 40.5% to 61.9%, 23.8% to 38.1%, and 42.9% to 57.1%, respectively. Qualitative analyses further show that Self-Harness does not simply add generic instructions, but effectively turns model-specific weaknesses into concrete, executable harness changes. These results suggest a path toward LLM-based agents that are not merely shaped by their harnesses, but can also participate in reshaping them.

</details>


### [13] [AGENTSERVESIM: A Hardware-aware Simulator for Multi-Turn LLM Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09613)
*Rakibul Hasan Rajib,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了AGENTSERVESIM，一个面向多轮LLM智能体服务的硬件感知模拟器，能够在普通CPU上以6%误差内复现真实系统的性能，避免了在昂贵加速器上进行重复部署的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮LLM智能体将服务从无状态请求处理转变为有状态程序执行，需要考虑程序级上下文的调度、KV缓存管理和路由策略。直接在真实系统上评估这些策略成本高昂，而现有LLM服务模拟器仅针对无状态请求级负载，忽略了智能体服务的核心动态（多轮程序执行、跨轮缓存局部性、工具调用间隙的KV驻留）。

Method: AGENTSERVESIM通过可组合模块实现程序粒度的服务策略评估：程序编排器（维护程序身份和轮次顺序）、工具模拟器（模拟工具调用间隙）、会话感知路由器（维护程序到实例的亲和性以实现缓存感知调度）、KV驻留模型（跨HBM、主机DRAM/CXL和驱逐策略追踪KV放置）。

Result: 在实际服务部署和硬件配置上，AGENTSERVESIM在所有关键性能指标上与真实系统行为的误差在6%以内，且完全在普通CPU上运行。

Conclusion: AGENTSERVESIM能够在不进行昂贵加速器部署的情况下，实现对智能体服务策略的可控、可重复探索。

Abstract: Multi-turn LLM agents interleave model calls with external tool invocations, shifting serving from stateless request processing to stateful program execution. Serving these workloads requires scheduling, KV-cache management, and routing policies that use program-level context, including turn dependencies, tool-induced gaps, and reusable KV state. Evaluating such policies directly on real systems is costly, since each design point may require dedicated accelerator time across arrival rates, model scales, serving-instance counts, and memory hierarchies. Simulation offers a scalable alternative, but existing LLM serving simulators target stateless request-level workloads and therefore omit the core dynamics of agent serving: multi-turn program execution, cross-turn cache locality, and KV-cache residency during tool gaps. We present AGENTSERVESIM, a hardware-aware simulator for multi-turn LLM agent serving. AGENTSERVESIM evaluates serving policies at program granularity through composable modules: a Program Orchestrator preserves program identity and turn order, a Tool Simulator materializes tool-induced gaps, a Session-Aware Router maintains program-to-instance affinity for cache-aware dispatch, and a KV Residency Model tracks policy-defined KV placement across HBM, host DRAM/CXL, and eviction. Across real serving deployments and hardware configurations, AGENTSERVESIM reproduces real-system behavior within 6% error across key performance metrics while running entirely on commodity CPUs. These results show that AGENTSERVESIM enables controlled, repeatable exploration of agent-serving policies without requiring exhaustive deployment on costly accelerators.

</details>


### [14] [Gradient-Guided Reward Optimization for Inference-time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09635)
*Hankun Lin,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出梯度引导奖励优化（GGRO），一种轻量级推理时对齐方法，通过梯度信号注入引导token来干预解码过程，提升LLM在分布漂移下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法（如Best-of-N、拒绝采样）依赖大量采样和奖励模型重排序，性能受限于基础模型生成质量且易受奖励黑客攻击。

Method: GGRO在解码时监控token级熵以识别高不确定区域，检测到分布漂移或未对齐时，利用现成奖励模型的梯度信号生成引导token，直接干预生成轨迹。

Result: 在安全、有用性和推理基准测试中一致提升推理时对齐性能，提高高质量响应覆盖率，增强对奖励黑客攻击的鲁棒性，计算开销极小。

Conclusion: GGRO是一种有效的轻量级推理时对齐方法，通过梯度引导的定向干预克服了传统采样密集型方法的局限。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) under distribution drift requires inference-time adaptation. While inference-time alignment methods such as Best-of-$N$ and rejection sampling are widely used, they frame the task as a sampling-intensive, reward-guided search, leading to two key limitations: their performance is bounded by the base model's generation quality, and their reliance on imperfect reward models makes them vulnerable to reward hacking. To address these challenges, we introduce Gradient-Guided Reward Optimization (GGRO), a lightweight inference-time method that performs targeted, minimal intervention during decoding via gradient guidance. Specifically, GGRO monitors token-level entropy to identify high-uncertainty regions indicative of drift or misalignment. Upon detection, it responds by injecting nudging tokens, generated using gradient signals from an off-the-shelf reward model, to steer the generation trajectory rather than merely re-ranking samples. Experiments show that GGRO consistently improves inference-time alignment across safety, helpfulness, and reasoning benchmarks. It also increases coverage of high-quality responses and robustness to reward hacking, with minimal computational overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/lhk2004/GGRO.

</details>


### [15] [End-to-End Context Compression at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09659)
*Ang Li,Sean McLeish,Haozhe Chen,Nimit Kalra,Zaiqian Chen,Artem Gazizov,Venkata Anoop Suhas Kumar Morisetty,Bhavya Kailkhura,Harshitha Menon,Zhuang Liu,Brian R. Bartoldson,Tom Goldstein,Sanae Lotfi,Micah Goldblum,Pavel Izmailov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Context Language Models (LCLMs)，通过编码器-解码器架构将长序列压缩为潜变量嵌入，在1:4至1:16压缩比下平衡了质量、速度和内存，改进了长上下文推理的效率-精度Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理受限于KV缓存随序列长度增长带来的内存瓶颈，现有KV缓存压缩方法要么严重降低模型质量，要么压缩单个长提示需要大量时间和计算，且许多方法与生产推理引擎不兼容。

Method: 通过架构搜索从零预训练多种变体以确定最佳设计，基于发现继续预训练一组0.6B编码器+4B解码器的模型（350B+ tokens），压缩比分别为1:4、1:8、1:16，使编码器将长token序列映射为短序列潜嵌入供解码器使用。

Result: LCLMs改进了通用任务性能、压缩速度和峰值内存使用的Pareto前沿，可作为长时程agent的高效骨干，支持扫描压缩上下文并按需自适应展开相关片段。

Conclusion: 重新审视并弥合了编码器-解码器压缩与KV缓存压缩之间的精度-效率差距，证明编码器-解码器方法在长上下文推理场景中具有竞争力。

Abstract: Long-context language model inference is bottlenecked by memory, as the KV cache grows with context length. Recent techniques to compress the KV cache fall short: they either degrade model quality substantially or require considerable time and compute to compress a single long prompt. Furthermore, many methods require the input to fit within the target model's context window, and are generally incompatible with modern production inference engines. Encoder-decoder compressors, which map a long token sequence to a shorter sequence of latent embeddings consumed by a decoder, are an appealing alternative in principle. However, existing approaches are not competitive with KV cache compression on the accuracy-efficiency frontier. In this work, we revisit encoder-decoder compression and close this gap. We first perform an architecture search, pre-training many variants from scratch to determine how best to design and train encoder-decoder compressors. Guided by our findings, we continually pre-train a family of 0.6B-encoder, 4B-decoder models on over 350B tokens each, at compression ratios of 1:4, 1:8, and 1:16. We introduce Latent Context Language Models (LCLMs), a family of compressors that improve the Pareto frontier across general-task performance, compression speed, and peak memory usage. We demonstrate that LCLMs serve as efficient backbones for long-horizon agents, letting the agent skim through a compressed long context and adaptively expand relevant segments on demand.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [16] [OmniMem: Perturbation-aware Memory Compression for Streaming Audio-Visual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07577)
*Guangzhi Sun,Yixuan Li,Yudong Yang,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniMem是一个为音视频大语言模型设计的内存高效流式推理框架，通过模态感知的内存分配和扰动感知的KV缓存选择，在不牺牲长视频理解能力的前提下大幅降低内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的音视频大语言模型在处理长视频时，视频token和KV缓存随长度线性增长，导致推理效率低下。已有的压缩方法对所有token一视同仁，忽略了视觉和音频模态之间严重的token数量不平衡问题。

Method: 1) 模态感知的内存分配策略：分别管理视觉和音频上下文，解决模态间token不平衡；2) 扰动感知的内存选择：保留信息丰富且非冗余的KV状态，实现紧凑内存；3) 预算感知的微调：在部署约束下鼓励模型将有用信息整合到保留的内存中。

Result: 在VideoMME Long、LVBench和LVOmniBench上，使用video-SALMONN 2+和Qwen-2.5-Omni评估。在相同内存预算下，OmniMem比强基线的训练无关压缩方法绝对准确率提升2-4%，微调后可额外提升1-2%。

Conclusion: OmniMem通过模态感知和扰动感知的KV缓存管理，有效解决了音视频LLM长视频推理的内存瓶颈，在压缩率与性能之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (LLMs) hold strong promise for long-form video understanding, yet their long-video inference is fundamentally limited by the linear growth of video tokens and key-value (KV) caches. We present OmniMem, a memory-efficient streaming framework designed specifically for audio-visual LLMs. Unlike existing compression methods that treat all tokens uniformly, OmniMem introduces a modality-aware memory allocation strategy that separately manages visual and audio contexts, addressing the severe token imbalance between the two modalities. OmniMem further preserves informative and non-redundant KV states through perturbation-aware memory selection, enabling compact memory without sacrificing long-range understanding. To strengthen compression under realistic deployment constraints, we also explore budget-aware fine-tuning, which encourages the model to consolidate useful information into retained memory. Experiments on VideoMME Long, LVBench, and LVOmniBench with video-SALMONN 2+ and Qwen-2.5-Omni show that OmniMem consistently improves over strong training-free compression baselines by 2-4% absolute accuracy under the same memory budgets, with an additional 1-2% gain after fine-tuning.

</details>


### [17] [Syll: Open-Source Personal Automation with Cross-Surface Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07594)
*Bo Zhang,Borui Zhang,Chenghao Jiang,Minglei Shi,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Syll 是一个开源、自托管的 multimodal agent 框架，统一了 MCP/API 工具、CLI 执行和视觉 GUI 控制，支持用户通过演示教学、双向交互、多模态证据审计以及外部化的记忆/技能/治理工件，已在 Photoshop、Audition、Stardew Valley、macOS Finder 等生产应用上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人 AI agent 大多只适配单一界面（API、CLI 或 GUI），缺乏对跨界面操作、用户教学和可审计性的支持。

Method: 提出 Syll 模块化运行时，统一 MCP/API 工具、CLI 执行和视觉 GUI 控制；核心是双向用户-agent 交互层：用户通过直接演示教学过程并编译为可复用技能，agent 执行结果转换为多模态证据（日志、关键帧、审批检查点）；将记忆、技能、例程和治理规则外部化为可编辑的本地工件。

Result: 在 Adobe Photoshop、Adobe Audition、Stardew Valley、macOS Finder 等桌面应用上验证；进行了面向机制的研究，验证了多模态路由、可教学的 GUI 回放和持久化本地工件。

Conclusion: Syll 可作为个人自动化的实用开源基础，让用户能够教学、检查和持续扩展。

Abstract: Personal AI agents must increasingly operate across APIs, shells, web surfaces, and desktop GUIs, yet many systems remain tuned to a single interface and offer limited support for user teaching and auditability. We present Syll, an open-source, self-hosted multimodal agent harness that unifies MCP/API tools, CLI execution, and visual GUI control in a modular runtime, enabling agents to coordinate computer use across heterogeneous interfaces while streamlining how users and agents exchange information. At the core of Syll is a bidirectional user-agent interaction layer: users teach procedures through direct demonstration, which Syll compiles into reusable skills; agent execution is translated back into multimodal evidence -- logs, keyframes, and approval checkpoints -- for inspection and control. Syll further externalizes memory, skills, routines, and governance as editable local artifacts, supporting straightforward inspection, extension, and downstream development. Our implementation has been validated on production desktop applications including Adobe Photoshop, Adobe Audition, Stardew Valley, macOS Finder and others. We report mechanism-oriented studies that validate multimodal routing, teachable GUI replay, and persistent local artifacts. We hope Syll can serve as a practical open-source foundation for personal automation that users can teach, inspect, and continuously extend.

</details>


### [18] [Where Instruction Hierarchy Breaks: Diagnosing and Repairing Failures in Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07808)
*Sanjay Kariyappa,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个白盒诊断框架，用于定位推理语言模型在指令层次结构中的失败模式（指令识别、冲突解决、响应实现），并基于此提出两种免训练的自我监控机制（并行输入监控器与顺序输出监控器），在不重新训练的情况下将规则遵循违规率降低81-99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅从端到端评估模型是否遵循指令层次结构，但非合规响应可能源于多种不同失败原因（未能识别相关指令、未能解决冲突、或推理正确但输出违规），缺乏细粒度诊断手段来定位具体失败环节。

Method: 1) 提出白盒诊断框架，将指令层次失败分解为指令识别、冲突解决、响应实现三个环节；2) 在长上下文版本的IHEval和IHChallenge上评估三个推理模型；3) 提出两种免训练自我监控机制：并行输入监控器（生成前低延迟冲突检测）和顺序输出监控器（响应级审查与修复）。

Result: 不同模型、任务和上下文长度下主导失败模式不同；最强的监控器（GPT-5.3）将规则遵循违规减少86%（静态攻击）和45%（自适应攻击）；整体违规降低81-99%。

Conclusion: 指令层次结构失败可被细粒度诊断和定位，免训练的自我监控机制能有效提升模型在代理工作流中的指令遵循能力，无需额外训练成本。

Abstract: Reasoning language models deployed in agentic workflows must follow an instruction hierarchy: when instructions from different sources conflict, the model should obey the highest-privilege applicable instruction. Existing benchmarks largely measure this behavior end-to-end, asking whether the final response is compliant. However, a non-compliant response can arise from several distinct failures: the model may fail to identify the relevant instructions in context, fail to resolve conflicts among identified instructions, or correctly resolve the conflict in its reasoning while still producing a violating response. We introduce a white-box diagnostic framework that localizes instruction hierarchy failures into instruction identification, conflict resolution, and response realization, making failures more interpretable. We evaluate three reasoning models--Gemma-4-31B-IT, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, and Claude Sonnet 4.6--on long-context adaptations of IHEval and IHChallenge, and find that the dominant failure mode varies across models, tasks, and context length. Building on the observation that models can often detect conflicts and output violations when explicitly prompted, we propose two training-free self-monitoring mechanisms: a parallel input monitor for low-latency conflict detection before generation, and a sequential output monitor for response-level review and repair. Across Gemma-4-31B-IT, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and GPT-5.3, the strongest monitor reduces rule-following non-compliance by 81-99%, with GPT-5.3 reductions of 86% under static attacks and 45% under adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [19] [Joint Structural Pruning and Mixed-Precision Quantization for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07819)
*Hoang-Loc La,Truong-Thanh Le,Amir Taherkordi,Phuong Hoai Ha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端联合优化框架，在同一搜索空间中同时学习结构化剪枝决策和混合精度量化策略，通过直接最小化全局误差传播而非逐层优化，显著提升大语言模型在超低比特（1-3比特）下的部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化(PTQ)方法逐层优化量化误差，忽略误差在网络中的累积和传播，且剪枝与量化通常孤立或顺序执行，导致次优解。亟需一种全局优化的联合压缩方法。

Method: 1) 提出混合精度PTQ策略，直接最小化整个模型的全局误差传播；2) 在此基础上开发联合优化方法，在同一搜索空间内同时学习结构化剪枝决策和混合精度量化策略。

Result: 超低比特（1-3比特）下：相比于SoTA权重-激活量化基线，WikiText困惑度降低最多21%；相比于SoTA仅权重量化方法，WikiText和C4困惑度分别降低59%和85%；相比于SoTA联合剪枝-量化方法，在超低比特下取得更优困惑度和推理性能。

Conclusion: 提出的全局误差感知联合优化框架在超低精度下显著优于现有方法，为LLM高效部署提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Recently, the efficiency of Large Language Models (LLMs) deployment has become a critical concern in practical applications. While post-training quantization (PTQ) and structural pruning are established techniques for reducing memory footprint and inference latency, most existing PTQ approaches optimize quantization errors on a per-layer basis, overlooking how errors accumulate and propagate through the network, often resulting in suboptimal solutions. Traditional pipelines also tend to apply pruning and quantization in isolation or sequentially, further compounding sub-optimality. We introduce a novel end-to-end framework that addresses these limitations in two key ways. First, we propose a novel mixed-precision PTQ strategy that directly minimizes global error propagation across the entire model, rather than isolating layer-wise errors. Building on this, we develop a novel joint optimization approach that simultaneously learns structural pruning decisions and mixed-precision quantization policies within a unified search space. Extensive experiments show that, at ultra-low precisions (1-3 bits), our quantization method reduces WikiText perplexity by up to 21% compared to state-of-the-art (SoTA) weight-activation quantization baselines. Against leading weight-only quantization methods, it achieves up to 59% and 85% lower perplexity on WikiText and C4, respectively. Compared to the SoTA joint pruning-and-quantization techniques, our proposed method delivers superior perplexity and reasoning performance at ultra-low bits.

</details>


### [20] [Contract2Tool: Learning Preconditions and Effects for Reliable Tool-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07904)
*Rahul Suresh Babu,Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Contract2Tool框架，从元数据、文档和执行轨迹中自动推断工具契约（前置条件、效果、风险等级、成本），用于因果工具过滤，减少大语言模型代理的Token消耗并保持高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动编写和维护工具契约无法扩展到大规模或变化的工具生态系统，需要自动化方法。

Method: Contract2Tool将工具的可观察证据（元数据、文档、执行轨迹）转换为规范化符号契约，支持内在评估并部署到下游因果工具过滤中。

Result: 学习契约的CMTF下游成功率达0.980（接近黄金契约的0.990），可见工具从100减至1，平均Token使用从26,172降至2,528。

Conclusion: 学习到的契约可在工具模式和可靠代理执行之间提供可扩展的契约层，兼顾效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model agents increasingly rely on external APIs, but standard tool schemas describe how to call a tool, not when the tool is causally appropriate or what task state it produces. Causal tool filtering addresses this gap by using lightweight contracts that specify each tool's preconditions, effects, risk level, and cost. However, manually writing and maintaining such contracts does not scale to large or changing tool ecosystems. We introduce Contract2Tool, a framework for inferring tool contracts from metadata, schemas, documentation, and execution traces. Contract2Tool converts observable tool evidence into normalized symbolic contracts that can be evaluated intrinsically and deployed inside downstream causal tool filtering. We evaluate learned contracts against gold preconditions, effects, and risk labels, and measure their downstream utility on multi-step agent tasks. Our results show that hybrid documentation-and-trace evidence produces contracts accurate enough to preserve most of the reliability and efficiency benefits of gold contracts. Learned-contract CMTF achieves 0.980 downstream success, close to 0.990 for gold-contract CMTF, while reducing visible tools from 100 to 1 and reducing average token usage from 26,172 to 2,528 relative to all-tools exposure. These results suggest that learned contracts can provide a scalable contract layer between tool schemas and reliable agent execution.

</details>


### [21] [MemToolAgent overview with a simple restaurant booking scenario where the agent retrieves similar memories, receives feedback on an invalid time format, and generates a reflection to update its memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07909)
*Suleyman Armagan Er,Danilo Ribeiro,Yogesh Virkar,Surafel Lakew,Adi Kalyanpur,James Gung,Thomas Delteil,Arshit Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MemToolAgent框架，通过记忆提取和检索模块改进LLM代理的工具使用能力，无需微调即可利用历史交互经验，在三个基准测试上分别取得29%、80%和17%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在需要利用长期历史经验进行工具调用时缺乏有效的记忆机制，而现有对话系统的记忆方案在工具使用场景下尚未被充分研究。

Method: 包含两个核心模块：(1) 记忆提取模块，将历史经验处理为结构化记忆条目，利用环境和用户反馈将错误执行蒸馏为评判性条目；(2) 检索模块，基于记忆相似度分布动态选择适量的记忆条目用于当前任务。

Result: 在WorkBench、NESTFUL和PEToolBench上分别比强基线提升29%、80%和17%。

Conclusion: 统一记忆条目格式和动态检索机制能有效提升代理的工具使用能力，且无需LLM微调。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) agents can use external tools to help users solve complex tasks. However, for problems that require learning from long-term historical events or from previous agent-environment interactions, LLM agents are required to use memory mechanisms to store and retrieve experiences. While sophisticated memory systems exist for dialogue agents, few studies have empirically examined how to improve agents' tool-using capabilities through past user-agent conversations. We propose MemToolAgent, a framework that improves tool use through memory management. Our approach contains a memory extraction module that processes past experiences into structured memory entries, and a retrieval module that dynamically selects a subset of the stored memory entries. This enables more personalized and accurate responses aligned with user preferences and feedback without requiring LLM fine-tuning. In summary, this work has three main contributions: (1) a unified memory entry format that improves both general-purpose and personalized tool use without LLM fine-tuning, (2) a reflection-based memory extraction that uses environment and user feedback to distill wrong executions into critiques to store, and (3) a retrieval module that chooses how many past experiences to use based on the memory similarity distribution. MemToolAgent achieves 29%, 80%, and 17% relative improvements compared to strong baselines on the WorkBench, NESTFUL, and PEToolBench benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [22] [Unification of Closed-Open Industrial Detection Scenarios: New Large-Scale Benchmarks,Challenges and Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07953)
*Zekai Zhang,Jinglin Zhang,Qinghui Chen,Gang Li,Da Chen,Shuainan Jing,He Wang,Dagang Li,Cong Liu,Cong Bai,Shengyong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了大规模多模态工业开放-闭合基准（MMIOC-1M），包含超过100万样本、14个超类、29个工业场景和351个缺陷子类别，同时提出了精炼文本-视觉提示网络（RTVPNet），通过专家辅助域投影、基于能量的稀疏采样和双向文本-视觉交互模块实现工业缺陷检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM在工业缺陷检测中面临两大限制：缺乏大规模多领域工业数据集，以及依赖手动提示（点、框、掩码）引入主观噪声且缺乏文本-视觉交互的细粒度理解能力。

Method: 1) 构建MMIOC-1M大规模多模态工业基准数据集；2) 提出RTVPNet，包含专家辅助域投影机制、能量基稀疏采样策略（自动生成视觉提示）和双向文本-视觉交互模块。

Result: RTVPNet在MMIOC-1M、LVIS和COCO基准上取得了最先进的性能，同时保持了计算效率。

Conclusion: MMIOC-1M是首个统一支持开放词汇和封闭集工业检测的大规模基准，RTVPNet有效解决了工业缺陷检测中的关键挑战。

Abstract: Large-scale Visual-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable success in natural visual tasks, yet their application to industrial defect detection remains challenging due to two fundamental limitations: (i) the scarcity of large-scale industrial datasets that cover diverse defect categories across multiple domains, and (ii) the reliance on manual prompts (points, boxes, masks) that introduce subjective noise and lack text-visual interaction for fine-grained understanding. To address these challenges, we introduce a Large-Scale Multi-Modal Industrial Open-Closed benchmark (MMIOC-1M) containing over one million samples across $14$ super-categories, $29$ industrial scenes, and $351$ defect subcategories. To our knowledge, MMIOC-1M is the first unified largest benchmark supporting both open-vocabulary and closed-set industrial detection, providing valuable pre-training data for LVLMs in industrial scenarios. Furthermore, we propose a Refined Text-Visual Prompt Network (RTVPNet) that incorporates three key innovations: (1) an expert-assisted domain projection mechanism that enables rapid adaptation of general vision models to industrial domains, (2) an energy-based sparse sampling strategy that automatically generates refined visual prompts without manual intervention, and (3) a bidirectional text-visual interaction module that enhances cross-modal semantic alignment and understanding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RTVPNet achieves state-of-the-art performance on MMIOC-1M, LVIS, and COCO benchmarks while maintaining computational efficiency. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/hellozzk/MMIO.

</details>


### [23] [VATS: Exploiting Implicit Authority in Error-Path Injection via Systematic Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07992)
*Harshil Patel,Kunal Pai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出VATS框架，发现通过MCP协议中的错误处理循环注入攻击向量，可使工具调用时的提示注入成功率提升至三倍（最高100%），结构定位（在错误上下文中夹带指令）是最有效的攻击方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议标准化了自主智能体的工具调用，但其错误处理循环存在未被充分研究的攻击面——工具错误消息具有隐式权威性，会触发智能体的修正推理模式，绕过标准安全机制。

Method: 提出VATS（工具流漏洞分析）框架，这是一个突变驱动的攻击方法，系统性地在7个结构性和语言性维度上演化对抗性载荷，在Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.5、GLM-5.1和Qwen3-Coder四个前沿模型上进行评估。

Result: 错误路径注入使标准间接提示注入（IPI）的成功率提升三倍，在受控评估中最高达到100%的遵从率；结构定位（在错误上下文中夹带指令）是所有测试模型中最有效的利用向量。

Conclusion: 生产框架的防护措施可以缓解部分漏洞，但模型层固有的敏感性对定制化智能体工作流构成了系统性风险。

Abstract: As the Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes tool-calling for autonomous agents, it introduces a critical, unexamined attack surface: the error-handling loop. We hypothesize that tool error messages possess implicit authority, triggering corrective reasoning modes that bypass standard safety heuristics. We introduce VATS (Vulnerability Analysis of Tool Streams), a mutation-driven framework that systematically evolves adversarial payloads across seven structural and linguistic dimensions. Our evaluation across four frontier models, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.5, GLM-5.1, and Qwen3-Coder, demonstrates that error-path injection triples the success rate of standard indirect prompt injection (IPI), achieving up to 100% compliance in controlled evaluations. We isolate structural positioning (sandwiching instructions within error context) as the most effective exploit vector across all tested models. While we find that production framework guardrails can mitigate these vulnerabilities, the inherent susceptibility of the model layer poses a systemic risk to bespoke agentic workflows.

</details>


### [24] [How Small Can You Go? LoRA Fine-Tuning 270M-8B Models for Merchant Information Extraction in Financial Transactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08051)
*Donghao Huang,Tomas Drietomsky,Benjamin Barrett,Zhaoxia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对24种小模型变体（从270M到8B参数）在金融交易结构化信息提取任务上进行系统评估，发现Qwen 3.5 4B模型在仅使用一半参数的情况下达到与LLaMA 3.1-8B相当的F1分数（96.60% vs 96.95%），0.8B的Qwen模型也能匹配大2.5-4倍模型的性能。研究还发现链式思考微调普遍提升0.3-1.8个F1点，但显式推理监督对结构化提取任务并非必要。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产系统中部署8B参数模型面临高昂的内存、延迟和成本约束，需要寻找更高效的替代方案。

Method: 系统评估24种模型变体（Gemma 3、Qwen 3.5、Aya、LLaMA 3.1四大系列），在金融交易商家信息提取任务上比较准确性、推理吞吐量、训练成本和硬件行为，并将14个微调后的子8B模型部署到Databricks Model Serving端点验证生产环境表现。

Result: Qwen 3.5 4B仅用一半参数达到96.60% F1（距8B基线0.35点）；0.8B Qwen达到94.75% F1匹配大2.5-4倍模型；链式思考微调提升0.3-1.8 F1点；推理监督对结构化提取非必要；生产部署后平均F1变化仅0.8点（Aya 3.35B除外下降3-5点）。

Conclusion: 提供了跨精度和延迟需求的部署建议，表明通过模型和训练策略选择可以在保持高准确性的同时大幅降低部署成本。

Abstract: Financial transaction processing requires extracting structured merchant information from noisy, abbreviated bank transaction strings at scale. Our current production system, a LoRA-fine-tuned LLaMA 3.1-8B, achieves 96.95% F1 on this task, but deploying 8-billion-parameter models imposes prohibitive memory, latency, and cost constraints. To identify more efficient alternatives, we conduct a deployment-focused study of 24 model variants spanning four model families: Gemma 3 (270M, 1B, 4B), Qwen 3.5 (0.8B, 2B, 4B), Aya (3.35B), and LLaMA 3.1-8B, systematically evaluating accuracy, inference throughput, training cost, and hardware behavior to assess production suitability. Our findings show that: (1) reproducing the LLaMA 3.1-8B fine-tune with a LoRA rank of 8 achieves 96.75% F1, only 0.20 points below the rank-32 baseline; (2) Qwen 3.5 4B with JSON-only prompting reaches 96.60% F1, within 0.35 points of the 8B baseline while using roughly half the parameters; (3) the 0.8B Qwen 3.5 model achieves 94.75% F1, matching models 2.5-4x larger and offering an attractive latency-accuracy trade-off; (4) chain-of-thought fine-tuning generally improves F1 by 0.3-1.8 points across most models, although Qwen 3.5 4B performs best with direct JSON-only prompting; and (5) Qwen 3.5 Think and Nothink training templates produce nearly identical results (F1 differences <0.004), indicating that explicit reasoning supervision is unnecessary for structured extraction tasks. We further deploy all 14 fine-tuned sub-8B models as Databricks Model Serving endpoints and observe that benchmark performance transfers reliably to production, with an average F1 change of only 0.8 points. Aya 3.35B, based on the Cohere2 architecture, is the sole exception, exhibiting a 3-5 point decline under serving conditions. Based on these results, we provide deployment recommendations across accuracy and latency requirements, ...

</details>


### [25] [Decision-Aware Memory Cards: Counterfactual-Inspired Context Selection and Compression for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08151)
*Xinyu Guan,Qianyang Zhao,Yuming Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CICL 是一个决策感知的上下文层，通过构建上下文图、多模型评分（Opus、Qwen、Codex/GPT-5.5等）和证据卡片打包，为预算有限的 LLM agent 提供高价值上下文选择，在 SWE-bench 检索任务上将 hit@1 从 0.58 提升至 0.78。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tool-using LLM agent 失败的原因往往不是缺少相关文本，而是决策时刻未能选择、压缩或呈现关键证据，现有方法缺乏可审计、可比较的上下文选择协议。

Method: CICL 设计一个上下文层：(1) 将实例证据组织为上下文图；(2) 通过八字段模式路由多模型（Opus、Qwen、Codex/GPT-5.5、Qwen-QLoRA）的判断；(3) 按行动偏移、结果提升、必要性和负迁移风险评分；(4) 将高价值证据打包为类型化记忆卡片供预算有限的 agent 使用。将决策信号与评分模型解耦，支持前沿模型、本地替代模型和轻量级排序器在统一可审计协议下比较。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 50 个文件检索实例上，Qwen3.6-plus 对 BM25 top-50 的重新排序将 hit@1 从 0.58 提升至 0.78，MRR@10 从 0.634 提升至 0.790。预算 120 时，CICL 在 v1 上达到 F1 0.620，v3 上为 0.425；移除 top-utility 语义 v3 单元后 F1 降至 0.000。补充实验包括 Qwen-QLoRA 在 710 个候选上的一致性、200 标签的 Opus 信号等。

Conclusion: CICL 提供了一个可复现的上下文测量与选择层，用于决策关键型上下文，而非端到端的编码 agent 修复方案。RepoBench-R 摘要仍优于卡片，紧凑型排序器尚未替代启发式方法。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents often fail not because relevant text is absent, but because decisive evidence is not selected, compressed, or surfaced at action time. We present CICL, a decision-aware context layer that turns instance evidence into a context graph, routes deterministic, Opus-assisted, Qwen, Codex/GPT-5.5, and Qwen-QLoRA judgments through a shared eight-field schema, scores units by action shift, outcome uplift, necessity, and negative-transfer risk, and packs high-utility evidence as typed memory cards for a budgeted agent. The design separates the measured decision signal from the judge model, so frontier annotation, local surrogates, and lightweight rankers can be compared under one auditable protocol. Empirically, CICL yields a concrete open-benchmark gain while exposing its limits. On 50 SWE-bench Verified file-retrieval instances, direct Qwen3.6-plus reranking of BM25 top-50 candidates raises hit@1 from 0.58 to 0.78 and MRR@10 from 0.634 to 0.790, with all 2,500 judgments parseable. Controlled diagnostics show action-criticality: at budget 120, CICL reaches F1 0.620 on v1 and 0.425 on v3, and removing the top-utility semantic v3 unit collapses F1 to 0.000. Supplementary checks add Qwen-QLoRA agreement over 710 candidates, a small 200-label real-code Opus-assisted signal, and a three-instance patch smoke validating retrieval-to-patch plumbing without claiming official SWE-bench success. RepoBench-R summaries still beat cards, and compact rankers do not yet replace the heuristic. CICL contributes a reproducible measurement and selection layer for decision-critical context, not an end-to-end coding-agent repair claim.

</details>


### [26] [SciTrace: Trajectory-Aware Safety Reasoning for Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08234)
*Tanush Swaminathan,Runmin Jiang,Letian Zhang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciTrace是一个将安全推理嵌入科学智能体流水线每个阶段的框架，通过安全内在推理循环(SIR)和组合工具链验证器(CTV)两种机制，在六个科学领域的240个高风险任务上实现了SOTA安全性，能捕获78.8%的单步监控遗漏的组合工具链风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM科学智能体的安全层与核心推理在结构上分离，导致两个失效模式：跨阶段的安全信号丢失，以及单个良性工具调用组合成有害结果而无法被单步过滤器检测。

Method: 提出SciTrace框架，包含两个互补机制：(1) 安全内在推理循环(SIR)，在思考者、实验者、写作者和审阅者各阶段维护累积风险状态，进行联合任务与安全推理；(2) 组合工具链验证器(CTV)，在执行前进行轨迹感知的安全检查，捕获多步工具序列中才显现的风险。

Result: 在六个科学领域的240个高风险研究任务和120个工具相关风险任务上，SciTrace在四个骨干模型上实现了SOTA安全性，一致提升了工具调用安全和对抗鲁棒性，同时保持科学输出质量；发现了78.8%的单步监控遗漏的组合工具链逃逸。

Conclusion: 将安全推理融入科学智能体流水线的每个阶段而非作为事后检查，能有效解决跨阶段信号丢失和组合性安全风险问题，在保证科学输出质量的同时显著提升安全性。

Abstract: LLM-based scientific agents have shown strong capacity for autonomous research, yet their safety layers remain structurally divorced from core reasoning: they inspect pipeline outputs rather than shaping the deliberation that produces them. This separation opens two failure modes: safety signals accumulated at one stage are discarded before the next, and sequences of individually benign tool calls can compose into harmful outcomes that no single-step filter detects. To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{SciTrace}, a framework that weaves safety reasoning into every stage of the scientific agent pipeline. SciTrace couples two complementary mechanisms: a \textit{Safety-Intrinsic Reasoning Loop} (SIR) that maintains a cumulative risk state across the Thinker, Experimenter, Writer, and Reviewer stages through joint task-and-safety deliberation, and a \textit{Compositional Tool-Chain Verifier} (CTV) that performs trajectory-aware safety checks before execution, catching risks that surface only across multi-step tool sequences. Evaluated on 240 high-risk research tasks and 120 tool-related risk tasks spanning six scientific domains, SciTrace achieves state-of-the-art (\textbf{SOTA}) safety among compared frameworks across four backbone models: it consistently improves tool call safety and adversarial robustness while preserving scientific output quality, and it uncovers \textbf{78.8\%} of the compositional tool-chain escapes that single-step monitors miss. The project website is available at https://opensciagent.github.io/SciTrace/.

</details>


### [27] [What Makes a Desired Graph for Relational Deep Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08491)
*Yao Cheng,Siqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究关系数据库到异构图转换中的图结构适配问题，发现直接从数据库Schema导出的图存在信息过载和语义碎片化两大问题，提出通过过滤（缓解信息过载）和注入（修复语义碎片化）两种操作进行结构优化，并开发了端到端的结构优化器，在26个任务上一致提升了准确率并降低了推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系深度学习将关系数据库转换为异构图供GNN使用，但直接从数据库Schema导出的图并不适合GNN的关系推理，存在系统性缺陷。

Method: 通过实证分析识别Schema导出图的两大问题（信息过载和语义碎片化），提出过滤（滤波）和注入两种结构适配操作，并开发端到端结构优化器自动应用这两种操作。

Result: 在26个分类、回归和推荐任务上，优化后的图一致提升了准确率，同时降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: 关系图深度学习需要经过结构适配而非直接使用原始Schema，过滤作为偏差-方差调节旋钮具有非单调效应，注入仅在显式恢复缺失的关系依赖时有效。

Abstract: Relational deep learning (RDL) converts relational databases (RDBs) into heterogeneous graphs, but graphs derived directly from database schemas are often not well suited for how graph neural networks (GNNs) perform relational reasoning. We study what makes a relational graph suitable for deep learning and show that schema-derived graphs suffer from two systematic failures: information overload and semantic fragmentation. Our empirical analysis reveals that the desired graph is not the raw schema, but a result of controlled structural adaptation. Performance depends on balancing two operations: mitigating information overload via filtering, and repairing semantic fragmentation via injection. Specifically, filtering serves as a bias-variance knob with non-monotonic effects, while injection improves performance only when it explicitly restores the relational dependencies missing from the original schema. Based on these findings, we develop an end-to-end structural optimizer that applies both operations to adapt relational graphs automatically. Across 26 tasks spanning classification, regression, and recommendation, the optimized graphs consistently improve accuracy while often reducing inference cost.

</details>


### [28] [AgentTrust: A Self-Improving Trust Layer for AI-Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08539)
*Chenglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种双存储自进化信任层（AgentTrust v2），用LLM裁判处理语义威胁（规则无法解决），并以裁判输出蒸馏确定性规则（处理词法威胁）和构建护证RAG记忆（处理语义威胁），使信任层在推理成本和安全性上持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent执行Shell命令、云操作等高风险行动时，需要一个信任层判断允许/警告/阻止/上报。已有规则包（如手写云规则）对词法级威胁有效，但对语义威胁（相同表面、不同意图）根本无能为力。需要一种能处理两类威胁且能自我进化的方案。

Method: 1) 将威胁分为词法（固定签名，规则可判定）和语义（意图依赖，规则不可判定）。2) 使用LLM裁判处理语义威胁。3) 裁判输出被蒸馏为确定性规则（针对词法威胁，降低成本）和护证RAG记忆（针对语义威胁，避免简单缓存导致的面孔坍塌）。4) 护证机制（corroboration guard）提升语义准确率+13pp。5) 在线回放中裁判调用率下降、领域准确率上升。

Result: 手写规则包在语义类别上的准确率提升为0pp；LLM裁判可将语义类别准确率提升至83.6-85.2%（近乎零误阻止）；护证RAG记忆使语义准确率+13pp（70→84）；在线45,000个动作的端到端回放中，裁判调用率从50%降至44%，领域准确率从71%升至80%，零良性硬阻止。

Conclusion: AgentTrust v2是一个自进化信任层：对词法威胁越来越便宜（蒸馏自己的规则），对语义威胁越来越智能（积累护证先例），并且永远不会硬阻止良性操作。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly take consequential actions -- shell commands, cloud operations, and arbitrary tool-calls -- so a trust layer must decide, per action, whether to allow, warn, block, or escalate. We argue that the right way to reason about such a layer is by threat type. Lexical (fixed-signature) threats, where danger lives in a stable token, are decidable by deterministic rules; semantic (intent-dependent) threats, where a benign and a malicious action share the same surface, are out of reach for rules by construction. We make this concrete with a negative proof: a determined, hand-authored cloud rule pack lifts held-out accuracy only 48 to 56% overall and moves the semantic categories by 0pp (data_db 29 to 29, observability 59 to 59, supply_chain 50 to 50), while a strong LLM judge carries exactly those categories. We give the judge a self-learning capability: on a corpus that is mainly semantic attacks it nearly doubles rule accuracy (48% to 83.6-85.2%) with near-zero false-blocks, and this holds across two model providers. We turn this into a self-improving dual-store system: the judge distills a growing deterministic rule floor on lexical threats (cheaper over time) and feeds a guarded RAG memory on semantic threats (a verdict-cache fails -- surface-twins collapse to ~58% -- so a corroboration guard lifts semantic accuracy +13pp, 70 to 84). The result is what sets AgentTrust v2 apart from its static v1 predecessor: a trust layer that self-evolves from its own stream of decisions -- cheaper on the lexical class (it distils its own rules) and smarter on the semantic class (it accrues guarded precedent), while never hard-blocking a benign action. An end-to-end online replay shows the judge-call rate falling (50% to 44%) and judge-domain accuracy rising (71% to 80%), with 0 benign hard-blocks across 45,000 actions.

</details>


### [29] [Q-Delta: Beyond Key-Value Associative State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08804)
*Sumin Park,Seojin Kim,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Q-Delta，一种查询感知的delta规则，将混合键-查询预测误差融入状态演化，在保持线性注意力效率的同时实现联合校正动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力方法将查询仅用于读出操作，与状态演化解耦，限制了模型表达能力。作者希望让查询参与状态更新过程以提升性能。

Method: 提出Q-Delta，一种查询感知的delta规则，将键-查询预测误差融入状态演化；给出了稳定性保证和硬件高效的chunkwise并行Triton实现。

Result: 在语言建模和长上下文检索任务上，相较于强基线方法实现了一致的性能提升，且保持竞争性吞吐量，优化稳定。

Conclusion: 查询感知的状态更新能够提升线性注意力的表现，Q-Delta在效率和效果之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Linear attention reformulates sequence modeling as recurrent state evolution, enabling efficient linear-time inference. Under the key-value associative paradigm, existing approaches restrict the role of the query to the readout operation, decoupling it from state evolution. We show that query-conditioned state readout induces a structured value prediction over accumulated memory that complements key-based retrieval. Based on this insight, we propose Q-Delta, a query-aware delta rule that integrates mixed key-query prediction errors into state evolution, enabling jointly corrective dynamics while preserving delta-rule efficiency. We establish stability guarantees for the resulting dynamics and derive a hardware-efficient chunkwise-parallel formulation with a custom Triton implementation. Empirical results demonstrate stable optimization, competitive throughput, and consistent improvements over strong baselines on language modeling and long-context retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [30] [STAR: Rethinking MoE Routing as Structure-Aware Subspace Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08814)
*Sumin Park,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STAR（结构感知路由），通过广义Hebbian算法学习输入的主子空间来增强MoE路由，使路由决策与输入结构对齐，实现更稳定的专家专业化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE路由通常使用浅层线性投影，对输入表示感知有限，导致路由不稳定，专家无法真正实现输入-专家专业化。

Method: 将MoE路由重新构想为子空间学习问题，在标准可学习路由基础上，通过广义Hebbian算法（GHA）扩展一个动态更新的主子空间，跟踪输入的主导结构。

Result: 在合成实验和大规模语言/视觉任务中，STAR一致提升了路由质量和下游性能；测试时可选的子空间更新进一步增强了对输入分布偏移的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结构感知的路由能实现稳定的专家专业化，是提升MoE效能的有效方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales model capacity efficiently by selectively routing inputs to a specialized subset of experts. However, input-expert specialization, the core motivation of MoE, critically depends on whether the router is actually aware of input structure. In practice, MoE routing is typically implemented as a shallow linear projection with limited awareness of input representation, which often leads to unstable routing. We propose STAR, a Structure Aware Routing that rethinks MoE routing as a subspace learning problem by augmenting standard learnable routing with an evolving principal subspace that tracks dominant input structure via Generalized Hebbian Algorithm (GHA). By aligning routing decisions directly with input structure, STAR enables stable expert specialization. We evaluate STAR on controlled synthetic setup and large-scale language and vision tasks, where it consistently improves routing quality and downstream performance over strong MoE baselines. Moreover, optional test-time subspace updates further enhance routing robustness and generalization under input distribution shifts.

</details>


### [31] [FAME: Forecastability-Aware Mixture of Experts for Heterogeneous Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08896)
*Qianyang Li,Xingjun Zhang,Shaoxun Wang,Tao Peng,Jia Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于稀疏混合专家模型的预测能力感知专家路由框架FAME，通过多维预测能力指纹和成本感知稀疏路由器，为每条时间序列动态选择少量适配的预测专家，在工业级零售数据集上比单一最强专家LightGBM降低12.4%的MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模零售和工业预测系统中包含大量异构时间序列，单一预测模型难以在所有场景下表现良好，而密集集成会增加推理成本且难以理解专家适配性。

Method: 提出FAME框架：1) 用多维预测能力指纹表示每条序列的数据特征；2) 从验证性能中挖掘专家适配性目标；3) 训练成本感知稀疏路由器，为每条序列激活少量预算内的专家。

Result: 在山东新北洋（SNBC）的5000+台机器、6000万+交易的生产规模数据集上，FAME Top-2相比最强单一专家LightGBM降低MSE 12.4%，平均每条序列执行1.92个专家。

Conclusion: 该框架将异构销售预测从启发式模型选择转变为预测能力模式与专家专业化的数据挖掘过程，并且已集成到补货规划管线中。

Abstract: Large-scale retail and industrial forecasting systems contain many heterogeneous time series whose lifecycle, sparsity, volatility, seasonality, spectral patterns, and contextual sensitivity differ substantially. A single forecasting model rarely performs well across all regimes, while dense ensembles increase inference cost and provide limited insight into expert suitability. This paper studies forecastability-aware expert routing: learning how data characteristics determine the suitability of forecasting experts. We propose \method{}, a sparse mixture-of-experts framework that represents each series with a multidimensional forecastability fingerprint, mines expert-suitability targets from validation performance, and trains a cost-aware sparse router to activate a small budgeted set of experts for each series. Using a production-scale vending-machine sales dataset from Shandong New Beiyang (SNBC), where the forecasting component has been integrated into the replenishment-planning pipeline, together with public retail benchmarks, we show that expert suitability varies systematically across data regimes. On the industrial dataset with 5,000+ machines and 60M+ transactions, \method{} Top-2 reduces MSE by 12.4\% over the strongest single expert, LightGBM, while executing 1.92 experts per series on average. The deployed component produces demand forecasts, while inventory-oriented gains are estimated by an offline replay simulator under a fixed replenishment policy rather than by online intervention. The framework turns heterogeneous sales forecasting from heuristic model selection into data mining of forecastability patterns and expert specialization. Code is available at https://github.com/hit636/FAME

</details>


### [32] [Order Matters: Unveiling the Hidden Impact of Macro Placement Sequences via Proxy-Guided LLM Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08904)
*Shibing Mo,Jing Liu,Jianchu Xu,Ruilin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OrderPlace框架，利用代理引导的LLM进化框架自动发现宏布局排序策略，通过轻量级代理评估机制解决序列评估成本问题，在ISPD 2005基准测试上相比SOTA方法减少14%-34%的线长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏布局中布局顺序长期被静态启发式规则主导，而早期次优决策会引发不可逆的连锁反应限制解空间，需要自动化发现更优排序策略。

Method: OrderPlace框架：使用LLM进化搜索代码级策略（从静态评分到动态物理启发机制），结合轻量级代理评估机制（确定性贪心探测）高效筛选候选策略。

Result: 在ISPD 2005基准测试上，相比WireMask-EA线长减少34.04%，相比SOTA方法EGPlace线长减少14.08%。

Conclusion: 布局顺序是可优化的关键维度，OrderPlace通过LLM进化框架自动发现了优于手工启发式的排序策略。

Abstract: Macro placement is a fundamental step in modern chip physical design, playing a crucial role in determining the solution quality of high-dimensional combinatorial optimization problems. Despite recent advancements in machine learning for spatial coordinate determination, the temporal dimension of placement sequencing remains largely governed by static heuristics. In this work, we demonstrate that the placement sequence is not merely a preprocessing step but a decisive factor in optimization, where suboptimal early decisions trigger irreversible domino effects that constrain the solution space. To harness this unexplored dimension, we propose \textbf{OrderPlace}, a proxy-guided LLM evolution framework for automatically discovering macro placement order strategies. Instead of relying on manually crafted heuristics such as area- or connectivity-based ordering, OrderPlace explores a broader space of code-level policies, ranging from static scoring metrics to dynamic physics-inspired mechanisms. To mitigate the prohibitive cost of evaluating sequences, we introduce a lightweight proxy evaluation mechanism that efficiently filters candidates using a deterministic greedy probe. Experimental results on the standard ISPD 2005 benchmarks demonstrate that OrderPlace discovers novel ordering strategies. Compared with WireMask-EA and the state-of-the-art method EGPlace, OrderPlace reduces wirelength by 34.04\% and 14.08\%, respectively.

</details>


### [33] [An Effective Router for Vision-Language Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08970)
*Can Wang,Shengwei Wang,Bolin Zhang,Zhiying Tu,Dianhui Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ARMS，一个用于视觉-语言模型（VLM）选择的轻量级路由器（800M参数），通过构建多模态数据集、增强VLM特征表示以及增量/独立训练策略，在VLM选择任务上超越GPT-4o等大规模商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对众多性能与资源需求各异的VLM，用户难以选择最合适的模型；现有路由方法主要针对语言模型，缺乏针对VLM选择的路由方案，主要面临缺乏专用数据、特征表示无效、模型空间僵化且适应成本高等挑战。

Method: 1) 构建包含7个主流VLM在32,626个图文查询上输出的多模态数据集；2) 提出ARMS，利用VLM画像增强输入信号，采用简单有效的架构改进查询和VLM能力的表示；3) 提出增量训练和独立训练两种扩展策略，支持快速适应新VLM。

Result: 在分布内和分布外测试集上均有效；ARMS（仅800M参数）通过扩展训练策略能适应更广泛的VLM空间，在VLM选择任务上击败大数百倍的GPT-4o等商业模型。

Conclusion: ARMS是一种高效、可扩展的VLM路由器解决方案，解决了VLM选择中的数据、表示和适应性问题，展示了小模型在模型路由任务上超越大规模模型的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) with varying performance and resource requirements are widely deployed, making it difficult for users to select the most appropriate one among numerous VLM candidates. Existing work reveals the performance paradox phenomenon in language models and focuses on routing methods to solve it. However, developing a router for VLM selection is still a critical yet challenging problem, which primarily faces: 1) lack of specialized data, 2) ineffective feature representation, and 3) rigid model space and costly adaptation. In this paper, we construct a multimodal dataset for VLM selection, containing the outputs of seven mainstream VLMs on 32,626 unique image-text queries. We then propose ARMS, a router for VLM selection. ARMS enhances input signals with VLM profiles, employs a simple but effective architecture to improve representations of queries and VLM capabilities. To improve ARMS' adaptation to new VLMs, we propose two extension training strategies: incremental training and independent training. Experimental results on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution test sets demonstrate the effectiveness of ARMS. In particular, using our training strategy, ARMs (only 800M in size) can adapt to a broader VLM space and defeat commercial models like GPT-4o that are hundreds of times larger in scale. Our code, models, and datasets are available in the anonymous repository.

</details>


### [34] [RTL-BenchLS: A Large-Scale Benchmark for RTL Reasoning and Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08976)
*Jing Wang,Shang Liu,Wenji Fang,Yuchao Wu,Yugao Zhu,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RTL-BenchLS是一个包含超过10,000个形式化验证的Verilog设计的大规模基准测试，涵盖比现有基准更大更复杂的设计。它提出了三种新任务（往返推理、掩码内容推理、仓库问题推理），通过形式化等价检查验证，无需手动测试平台。评估8个LLM，最佳模型仅达到12-28%正确率，远未饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL基准测试在规模和任务范围上存在固有限制：设计规模小且简单，任务几乎仅聚焦于spec-to-RTL生成，前沿模型已经饱和。扩大基准规模面临根本性困难，因为需要对齐的标签（如规范和测试平台），而真实世界设计很少提供这样的高质量对齐数据。

Method: 构建超过10,000个形式化验证的Verilog设计的大规模基准。提出三种自监督新任务（往返推理、掩码内容推理、仓库问题推理），通过形式化等价检查进行验证，无需手动测试平台，从而解决扩展瓶颈。

Result: 评估8个LLM，最佳模型在自然语言往返推理上仅达23%，掩码内容推理28%，仓库问题修复12%。现有基准上的模型饱和问题在RTL-BenchLS上不复存在。

Conclusion: RTL-BenchLS比现有基准更具挑战性，为LLM在硬件设计自动化中的应用留下了充足的改进空间，并提供了未来发展的指导。

Abstract: LLM-based RTL generation and reasoning is a promising direction for hardware design automation. High-quality benchmarks are critical infrastructure for tracking progress in this direction. However, existing RTL benchmarks face inherent limitations in both scale and task scope. The designs they cover are typically small and simple, and the tasks focus almost entirely on specification-to-RTL generation. Frontier models' performance already saturates on the existing benchmarks. Scaling these benchmarks up is fundamentally difficult because aligned labels are required for benchmarking, such as specifications and testbenches. Such aligned high-quality data are rarely available for real-world designs. We introduce RTL-BenchLS, a large-scale benchmark addressing both limitations above. It contains over 10,000 formally verified Verilog designs, covering substantially larger and more complex designs than existing benchmarks. Beyond specification-to-RTL generation, we propose three novel tasks that jointly evaluate reasoning and generation: round-trip reasoning, masked-content reasoning, and repository-issue reasoning. The first two are self-supervised, which directly resolves the scaling bottleneck. All tasks are verified through formal equivalence checking without any manual testbenches. We evaluate eight LLMs on RTL-BenchLS. Even the best model reaches only 23% on natural-language round-trip reasoning, 28% on masked-content reasoning, and 12% on repository-issue fixing. RTL-BenchLS is substantially more challenging than existing benchmarks. It leaves ample room for future improvement and offers guidance for developing LLM-based methods for hardware design.

</details>


### [35] [REFLECT: Intervention-Supported Error Attribution for Silent Failures in LLM Agent Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09071)
*Xiaofeng Lin,Yingxu Wang,Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Daniel Guo,Sahil Arun Nale,Charles Fleming,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TraceCare方法，通过诊断候选错误步骤、使用特定补丁进行受控重放测试，并将验证结果作为对比证据来细化最终归因，从而在LLM Agent的长计划-执行轨迹中定位静默错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法将干预结果反馈回来细化归因本身，导致LLM Agent在复杂任务的长轨迹中难以定位静默错误。

Method: 诊断候选错误步骤 → 通过诊断特定补丁进行受控重放 → 使用验证结果翻转作为对比证据来细化最终归因。

Result: 在四个多跳推理基准上，该方法在所有同类审计方法中取得了最高的定位准确率，在结构化工具使用轨迹上提升最大。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅能提供可操作的错误定位，甚至在无法获取ground-truth答案时依然有效。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now solve complex tasks through long plan-and-execution traces, yet the ability to locate errors in a completed traces still lags far behind, especially in the \emph{silent failure} regime. Existing approaches predict suspect steps via classifiers or LLM judges, or recover correct answers via retry, but none feed the intervention outcome back to \emph{refine the attribution itself}. We propose \methodname, a method that closes this gap by diagnosing a candidate error step, testing it through controlled replay with a diagnosis-specific patch, and using the verified outcome flip as contrastive evidence to refine the final attribution. Across four localization benchmarks spanning multi-hop reasoning across domains, \methodname achieves the highest localization accuracy among same-auditor methods across all four benchmarks, with the largest gains on structured tool-use traces, while providing actionable localization even when ground-truth answers are unavailable.

</details>


### [36] [Late-Layer Fusion is Enough: Dual-Path Vision Token Routing for Multimodal Large Language Models under Visual Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09131)
*Siyuan Liu,Jinyang Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DPVR-LF，一种针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的非对称路由框架。通过分析发现视觉token在中间层即饱和，因此将视觉token在饱和点路由到一层可训练的侧分支，深层Transformer仅处理文本token，仅在最后一层重新融合视觉和文本流。仅需约3%可训练参数即可保持竞争性能，同时大幅减少视觉计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs继承对称Transformer结构，对图像和语言token施加相同计算，忽视了模态间信息密度、冗余度和所需推理深度的显著差异。观察到视觉token在中间层即饱和，而文本token需要深层语义处理，这种架构对称性与模态深度异步演化之间存在错配，导致视觉计算冗余。

Method: DPVR-LF：在视觉token饱和点将其路由到一层可训练侧分支，深层Transformer堆栈仅处理文本token（跳过图像位置），仅在最后一层重新融合视觉和文本流。整体参数量仅增加约3%。

Result: 在标准基准测试上保持竞争性的多模态性能，同时显著减少深层Transformer堆栈中的视觉计算量。

Conclusion: 挑战了视觉token必须遍历所有深层语言模型层的传统假设，表明单层后期融合足以维持LLaVA风格MLLM的强感知能力。
