Method: 将 HuggingFace Llama 模型编译为单个持久协作式 CUDA kernel（一次 launch 跑完整前向）；使用冻结调度 IR 验证器静态检查死锁和竞态安全；基于同一份代码库自动 retarget 到不同 GPU 架构；支持 W8A16 精度并通过搜索优化 int8 megakernel。

Result: 在 7,160 个对抗性调度中零误报，接受全部 360 个真实 lowerings；在 10/10 模型上正确生成 megakernel；SmolLM2-135M 上复现 HuggingFace 贪心解码（perplexity 差异 2.5e-7）；推理级显卡上 L4 提升 1.33x、L40S 1.25-1.27x、A10G 1.08x、RTX 5090 1.19-1.23x；训练级显卡 A100/H100 上落后。

Conclusion: AMK 展示了全自动、安全验证的 megakernel 编译系统在推理级显卡上的性能优势，同时指出了训练级显卡上跨 SM 同步的瓶颈，为后续优化提供了方向。

Abstract: AutoMegaKernel (AMK) compiles a HuggingFace Llama-family model into a single persistent cooperative CUDA kernel that runs the whole forward pass in one launch, with no per-model hand-written CUDA. The contribution is the system, not raw speed.
  A frozen schedule-IR validator statically certifies deadlock-freedom and race-freedom via static graph checks (not a mechanized proof), so an unsafe agent-proposed schedule is rejected before launch: across 7,160 adversarial schedules (6,091 unsafe) it had zero false-accepts and accepted all 360 real lowerings. The same source retargets sm_80/sm_90/sm_120 from one codebase, auto-generates correct megakernels for 10 of 10 supported models, and on a real SmolLM2-135M checkpoint reproduces HuggingFace greedy decode token-for-token (perplexity match 2.5e-7). An unattended, agent-drivable autoresearch loop self-improves the megakernel over its own baseline (1.25-1.72x).
  A search-found int8 (W8A16) megakernel beats CUDA-graphed cuBLAS bf16 at batch-1 decode across NVIDIA's datacenter inference fleet: L4 up to 1.33x, the current-gen L40S 1.25-1.27x, A10G up to 1.08x at scale, and the consumer RTX 5090 1.19-1.23x. The ordering is not a clean function of bandwidth (the 864 GB/s L40S beats the 600 GB/s A10G); the divide is inference-class vs training-class. AMK trails cuBLAS on the high-bandwidth training-class A100/H100, where the harness localizes the cross-SM-sync bottleneck; we report the gap plainly. This is a precision-asymmetric (W8A16 vs bf16) comparison at decode position 0; the largest real checkpoint is TinyLlama-1.1B. Code and the harness: https://github.com/RightNow-AI/AutoMegaKernel

</details>


### [72] [LEAF: Growing Trees Without Branching for Speech-Aware Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07610)
*Argyrios Gerogiannis,Yekaterina Yegorova,Mark Hasegawa-Johnson,Venugopal V. Veeravalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEAF方法，通过回顾式树结构学习改进GRPO在语音大模型后训练中的细粒度信用分配，无需在线分支或额外解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO风格的语音大模型后训练方法对所有token分配相同的终端奖励，忽略了语音条件补全中共享前缀后有分支决策的结构信息，导致粗粒度的信用分配问题。

Method: LEAF方法：采样完整响应→选择高惊讶度边界→按共享前缀分组响应→使用后代奖励分配span级优势。基于低秩适配和回顾式树结构，无需在线分支。

Result: 在语音问答和语音翻译基准上，相同rollout和低秩适配预算下优于GRPO，且更小的LEAF模型超越当前全参数微调的SOTA基线。

Conclusion: LEAF通过回顾式树结构恢复批内结构信息，实现更精细的span级信用分配，显著提升语音大模型后训练效果。

Abstract: State-of-the-art GRPO-style methods for speech-aware large language model post-training suffer from coarse credit assignment, broadcasting the same terminal-reward advantage to every token in a response. This ignores useful structure within rollout batches, where speech-conditioned completions often share prefixes before diverging at important decisions. We propose Low-rank Exploration with Adaptive Forking (LEAF), a retrospective tree-based RL method that recovers this structure without online branching or additional decoding. LEAF samples complete responses, selects high-surprisal boundaries, groups responses by shared prefixes, and assigns span-level advantages using descendant rewards. We theoretically justify LEAF's span-level credit assignment and boundary-selection design. Empirically, LEAF improves over GRPO across speech question answering and speech translation benchmarks under the same rollout and low-rank adaptation budget. Notably, smaller LEAF-trained models outperform current state-of-the-art, full-parameter baselines.

</details>


### [73] [ScaleSweep: Accurate NVFP4 Post-Training Quantization of LLMs via Block Scale Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07618)
*Li Lin,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ScaleSweep是一种针对NVFP4硬件支持的4位量化格式的块尺度优化方法，通过扫描可行的块尺度候选值并选择最小化目标函数的值来提升量化精度，在Llama和Qwen模型上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NVFP4尺度初始化方法主要依赖AbsMax，与最优解之间存在明显差距，需要更优的尺度优化方法。

Method: 提出ScaleSweep方法，扫描可行的块尺度候选值并选择最小化目标函数的候选值；同时给出了NVFP4量化的理论分析，推导了基于MSE和WMSE的扫描范围上下界，大幅缩减搜索空间。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen模型上，ScaleSweep持续优于现有初始化方法，在激进的全端到端量化下（权重、激活、KV缓存和查询状态），保留了全精度性能的93%以上。

Conclusion: ScaleSweep是一种简单高效的NVFP4尺度优化方法，能有效缩小与全精度的差距，且开销可忽略不计。

Abstract: NVFP4 is a recently introduced hardware-supported FP4 format that improves the fidelity of 4-bit quantization through fine-grained block scales. However, existing NVFP4 scale initialization methods still primarily rely on AbsMax initialization, which leaves a noticeable gap to the optimal solution. To address this, we propose ScaleSweep, a simple and efficient scale optimization method that sweeps over feasible block scale candidates and selects the candidate that minimizes a target objective. We further provide a theoretical analysis of NVFP4 quantization and derive both lower and upper bounds for the required sweep range under mean square error (MSE) and weighted mean square error (WMSE) between the original tensor and the quantized reconstructed tensor. The proposed bounds substantially reduce the sweep space while preserving the optimal candidate, enabling negligible overhead compared with the baseline quantization operators. Experiments on Llama and Qwen models demonstrate that ScaleSweep consistently improves quantization performance over existing initialization methods and further narrows the gap to full precision. In particular, under aggressive end-to-end quantization of weights, activations, KV cache, and query states, ScaleSweep preserves more than 93% of the full-precision performance.

</details>


### [74] [PIPE-Cypher: Automatic Enterprise Benchmark Generation for Text-to-Cypher Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08481)
*Suraj Ranganath,Anish Raghavendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIPE-Cypher是一个本地化流水线，能够将实时的属性图以及可选的种子查询（来自用户问题、分析日志或智能体工具调用）自动转换为平衡的NL-to-Cypher基准测试集。它结合了模式分析、逆向查询接地、约束生成、确定性Cypher治理、执行验证、脱敏、多样性控制和经过校准的本地LLM评判器，支持基准测试随图结构、用户和工作负载的演化而重复更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业属性图的模式结构、术语、领域假设、治理约束和用户交互模式高度异构，现有Text2Cypher基准难以反映真实部署场景中用户和智能体实际提出的问题。创建这样的基准非常困难，因为模式和值是唯一的，图结构随时间变化，且每个NL-查询对必须可执行、使用真实实体、保持多样性并在查询类型和难度上平衡。

Method: PIPE-Cypher构建了一个完整的本地基准生成流水线，包含以下核心步骤：模式分析（schema profiling）、逆向查询接地（reverse-query grounding）、约束生成（constrained generation）、确定性Cypher治理（deterministic Cypher governance）、执行验证（execution validation）、脱敏处理（redaction）、多样性控制（diversity controls），以及使用Qwen3.5-9B作为本地LLM进行生成和评判，并对评判器行为进行人工标注校准。

Result: PIPE-Cypher生成了3,000个可接受的FinBench/SNB示例，完成了三个消融实验套件，使用人工标签校准了评判器行为，并评估了11个本地下游模型。结果表明零样本迁移效果较弱，而少量样本控制显示模式特定的示例库可以帮助兼容的模型族。

Conclusion: PIPE-Cypher使Text2Cypher基准测试成为一个可重复的过程，能够随图结构、用户和目标工作负载的演化而持续更新。该基准刻意具有区分度，能够有效评估模型在特定企业知识图谱上的真实表现。

Abstract: Enterprise property graphs vary widely in schema structure, internal terminology, domain assumptions, governance constraints, and user interaction patterns. A deployment-relevant Text2Cypher benchmark therefore reflects the questions users and agents actually ask of that graph. Creating such a benchmark is difficult because schemas and values are unique, and graph structure changes over time. Each NL-query pair must also be executable, use real graph entities, preserve diversity, and remain balanced across query types and difficulty levels. We present PIPE-Cypher, a local benchmark-generation pipeline that turns a live property graph and optional seed queries from customer questions, analyst logs, or agent tool calls into balanced NL-to-Cypher benchmarks. PIPE-Cypher combines schema profiling, reverse-query grounding, constrained generation, deterministic Cypher governance, execution validation, redaction, diversity controls, and a calibrated local LLM judge. Using local Qwen3.5-9B generation and judging, PIPE-Cypher exports 3,000 accepted FinBench/SNB examples, completes three audited ablation suites, calibrates judge behavior with human labels, and evaluates 11 local downstream models. The resulting benchmark is deliberately discriminative: zero-shot transfer is weak, while a few-shot control shows that schema-specific example banks can help compatible model families. Together, PIPE-Cypher makes Text2Cypher benchmarking a repeatable process that evolves with the graph, its users, and its target workloads.

</details>


### [75] [Evaluation of ML Resource Utilization Requires Model Life Cycle Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07632)
*Jared Fernandez,Clara Na,Yonatan Bisk,Constantine Samaras,Emma Strubell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该立场论文提出应将生命周期评估（LCA）应用于AI系统开发与部署的全流程，以准确核算从硬件制造到训练推理的能耗与环境影响，超越传统的单次训练或单次推理效率评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI效率评估方法（如关注单次训练或单次推理的成本）已不足以反映AI系统全生命周期的真实能源需求与环境影响，随着AI流水线和基础设施日益复杂，需要更全面的核算方法。

Method: 本文为立场论文，主张将生命周期评估（LCA）方法论引入机器学习模型开发与部署的全流程，涵盖物理计算硬件的隐含成本以及训练和推理的运营成本。

Result: 提出了将生命周期评估应用于AI系统全生命周期（从硬件到训练再到推理）的框架需求，以更准确地核算资源消耗和下游影响。

Conclusion: 为了研究人员、开发者、政策制定者和用户能够正确评估构建大规模AI系统的障碍，必须采用生命周期评估方法来全面核算AI系统的能源需求和环境影响。

Abstract: Proper accounting of the energy requirements and environmental impact of artificial intelligence (AI) systems is necessary for researchers, developers, policy makers, and users to assess the barriers to building systems at scale. With the growing complexity of pipelines and underlying infrastructure needed to develop and deploy AI systems, previous approaches for evaluating AI efficiency which focus on the costs of a single training run or an individual inference prediction are no longer sufficient. In this position paper, we enunciate the need for applying life cycle assessment to evaluate the costs of the machine learning model development and deployment pipeline to properly account for the required resources and downstream impact. Life cycle assessments enable the incorporation of costs across the full life cycle of an AI system and its underlying infrastructure, from the embodied costs associated with the physical computing hardware through the operational costs in training and inference.

</details>


### [76] [Semantic Cache Distillation: Efficient State Transfer via Reuse and Selective Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07684)
*Qianli Ma,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Zhi Yao,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义缓存蒸馏(SCD)框架，用紧凑语义编码替代原始KV缓存传输，通过Reuse（低秩子空间重构）和Patch（稀疏过渡层归一化输入预测）两种机制，在带宽受限的LLM推理场景中实现2.65倍TTFT加速，同时将生成质量控制在F1 5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离式架构中KV缓存传输造成严重通信瓶颈，TTFT过长；且跨异构模型复用缓存导致语义错配逐层累积，降低生成质量。

Method: SCD框架包含两个机制：(1) Reuse——从低秩子空间重构大部分层以最小化传输成本；(2) Patch——在稀疏过渡层预测归一化输入以截断错误传播。

Result: 相比oracle consumer预填充，SCD实现最多2.65倍TTFT加速；在带宽受限场景的质量-延迟Pareto前沿上优于量化和选择性重计算基线；生成质量保持在oracle F1的5%以内。

Conclusion: SCD通过语义级缓存蒸馏有效缓解了分离式LLM推理中的通信瓶颈和跨模型复用质量下降问题。

Abstract: Disaggregated serving alleviates memory bottlenecks in Large Language Model (LLM) inference but creates a severe communication bottleneck: transmitting high-dimensional Key-Value (KV) caches often dominates time-to-first-token (TTFT). Moreover, reusing caches across heterogeneous models (e.g., base and fine-tuned variants) causes semantic misalignment that accumulates over layers, degrading generation quality. We propose Semantic Cache Distillation (SCD), a loss-constrained framework that replaces raw KV transmission with compact semantic codes. SCD addresses these challenges via two mechanisms: (1) Reuse, which reconstructs most layers from low-rank subspaces to minimize transfer cost, and (2) Patch, which predicts normalized inputs at sparse transition layers to truncate error propagation. Empirically, SCD delivers up to 2.65 $\times$ TTFT speedup over the oracle consumer prefill and dominates quantization and selective recomputation baselines on the quality--latency Pareto frontier in bandwidth-constrained regimes, while keeping generation quality within 5\% F1 of the oracle.

</details>


### [77] [HARP: Efficient Data Selection for Finetuning Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07690)
*Ning Wang,Zhengxin Zhang,Maosen Tang,Yitang Gao,Claire Cardie,Sainyam Galhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARP是一种高效的基于训练的微调数据选择方法，通过层次化活动区域剪枝和贝叶斯推断，在保持下游对齐的同时大幅降低选择成本，相比最强基线提升最多+8.9个点且只需约1/7的训练样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调数据选择方法中，免训练的选择器可扩展但使用代理指标可能不匹配目标；基于训练的选择器能更好反映下游效用但需要大量昂贵的训练-评估迭代。需要一种既保持下游对齐又降低选择成本的方法。

Method: HARP将训练池组织成节点-叶子层次结构，仅评估代表性叶子节点，用经验贝叶斯后验推断未测量的效用，并通过两种互补包络选择数据：HARP-C保守控制冗余，HARP-E加性奖励互补区域。

Result: 理论证明了在局部平滑和有界估计误差下HARP能控制选择误差并降低训练-评估成本。实验上HARP变体取得最佳结果，比最强基线高出最多+8.9个点，同时仅使用约1/7的训练样本。

Conclusion: HARP是一种高效的基于训练的数据选择方法，能在显著降低计算成本的同时保持甚至提升下游任务性能。

Abstract: Finetuning data selection requires balancing two competing goals: selecting examples that improve the downstream objective, and doing so without repeatedly finetuning models. Train-free selectors are scalable but rely on proxies such as embedding similarity or clustering, which may not match the target objective. Train-based selectors better reflect downstream utility through gradient signals, subset evaluation, or Shapley attribution, but require many costly train--evaluate iterations. We propose Hierarchical Active Region Pruning (HARP), an efficient train-based selector that preserves downstream alignment while reducing selection cost. HARP organizes the training pool into a node--leaf hierarchy, evaluates only representative leaves, and infers unmeasured utilities with empirical Bayes posteriors. It then selects data using two complementary envelopes: HARP-C, which conservatively controls redundancy, and HARP-E, which additively rewards complementary regions. We theoretically show that, under local smoothness and bounded estimation error, HARP controls selection error while reducing train--evaluate cost. We further validate that HARP variants achieve the best result and outperform the strongest baseline by up to $+8.9$ points, while using roughly $7\times$ fewer training examples.

</details>


### [78] [How Much Dense Attention is Necessary? Oracle-Guided Sparse Prefill for Full/GQA Layers in Hybrid Long-Context Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07703)
*Hongxing Wang,Harenome Razanajato,Zhen Zhang,Yujie Yuan,Hongsheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究长上下文预填充中密集注意力是否必要，提出注意力质量top-k oracle方法诊断稀疏预算可行性，并通过KL蒸馏训练辅助索引器实现稀疏服务，在Qwen系列模型上验证了质量保持与加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文预填充成本高昂，即便在混合模型中，全/GQA层仍需评分历史序列。作者希望探究在显式支持粒度和top-k预算下，需要多少密集注意力才能保持任务级行为。

Method: 对现有GQA检查点提出注意力质量top-k oracle：对每层每查询位置计算密集注意力，选取头平均后的token支持集并仅对该支持集重新计算注意力。基于oracle指导，通过KL蒸馏从密集注意力质量分布训练头折叠辅助索引器，保持骨干网络冻结。

Result: 在Qwen系列检索密集型评估中，oracle长查询行与密集基线差距在1分以内；Qwen3.5-9B的4K到100K RULER扫描差距在0.48分以内。蒸馏索引器在16K/32K验证宏平均差距分别为+2.04和+1.13分。单卡TTFT加速比分别为1.71x（NPU）和1.93x（GPU），随机初始化可达3.44x。

Conclusion: 长上下文预填充中密集注意力可以被稀疏化，oracle方法证明存在可观稀疏预算，蒸馏索引器可部分实现该预算，但完全匹配的质量-延迟前沿留待未来工作。

Abstract: Long-context prefill remains expensive because full/GQA layers still score the historical sequence, even in hybrid models with local, sparse, linear, or recurrent components. We study how much dense attention is needed to preserve task-level behavior under explicit support granularity and top-k budgets.
  We introduce an attention-mass top-k oracle for existing GQA checkpoints: for each layer and query position, it computes dense attention, selects head-averaged token support, and recomputes attention only on that support. The oracle is a diagnostic reference, not a deployable accelerator, and separates sparse-budget feasibility from indexer error and runtime realization effects.
  On Qwen-family retrieval-heavy evaluations, the longest per-query oracle rows stay within 1 point of dense, and a Qwen3.5-9B RULER-style sweep from 4K to 100K stays within 0.48 points. Guided by the oracle, we derive a head-collapsed auxiliary indexer trained by KL distillation from dense attention-mass distributions while keeping the backbone frozen. With separately distilled Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-9B indexers, the reported 16K/32K validation macro gaps are +2.04 and +1.13 points, treated as quality preservation rather than improvement; fused selection-block-shared support can introduce a larger realization gap.
  Preliminary single-card TTFT measurements show distilled-indexer sparse serving speedups of 1.71x for Qwen3.5-0.8B on NPU and 1.93x for Qwen3.5-9B on GPU against its dense FlashAttention-2 baseline. Additional random-init stress rows reach 3.44x, indicating sparse-runtime headroom but not validated output quality. This first release separates oracle feasibility, distilled-indexer quality, and runtime headroom, leaving a fully matched quality-latency frontier to future work.

</details>


### [79] [WhiFlash: Accelerating Speculative Decoding with Token-Level Cross-Paradigm Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07710)
*Young D. Kwon,Miles Williams,Rui Li,Alexandros Kouris,Stylianos I. Venieris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WhiFlash 是一种跨范式的投机解码方法，统一了自回归和基于扩散的并行草稿模型，通过细粒度令牌级控制器和缓存管理优化，显著提升LLM推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法使用静态草稿范式（自回归用于推理，扩散用于结构化输出），但草稿精度在同序列内波动剧烈，静态范式和粗粒度路由无法充分利用性能潜力。

Method: 提出WhiFlash，统一自回归和扩散并行草稿的统一投机解码方法，包含：1) 细粒度路由机制（轻量级熵策略或学习型神经策略）；2) Lazy Catch-up和KV-only Prefill两种缓存管理优化，将切换开销降至每轮延迟的7%以下。

Result: 相比最新自回归方法EAGLE-3，吞吐量提升最高69.6%；相比基于扩散的DFlash，吞吐量提升最高37.3%。

Conclusion: 利用不同草稿架构的互补优势，WhiFlash实现了更高的接受长度和显著的吞吐量提升，验证了跨范式投机解码的有效性。

Abstract: The autoregressive nature of large language models (LLMs) remains a significant bottleneck for inference, particularly in complex agentic workloads. While speculative decoding (SD) accelerates inference, current approaches rely on static drafting paradigms, utilising either autoregressive drafting models for reasoning or diffusion-based parallel drafting models for structured outputs. We empirically find that drafting accuracy fluctuates dramatically within a single sequence, leaving significant performance unrealised by static paradigms and coarse-grained routing. To address this volatility, we introduce WhiFlash, the first cross-paradigm SD method that unifies autoregressive and diffusion-based parallel drafting under a single token-level controller. WhiFlash adopts a fine-grained routing mechanism that employs either a lightweight entropy-based or a learned neural policy, both parametrised to provide a tunable balance between expected token gain and latency. To make high-frequency switching computationally viable, we introduce novel cache-management optimisations, Lazy Catch-up and KV-only Prefill, reducing switching overhead to below 7% of per-round latency. By capitalising on the complementary strengths of fundamentally distinct drafting architectures, WhiFlash achieves significantly higher acceptance lengths, yielding category-specific throughput gains of up to 69.6% over the state-of-the-art autoregressive EAGLE-3 and 37.3% over the diffusion-based DFlash.

</details>


### [80] [Rosetta Memory: Adaptive Memory for Cross-LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07711)
*Hao Yang,Shiqi Shen,Haoxuan Li,Zhipeng Wang,Zhi Gong,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从"以LLM为中心的内存设计"转向"以内存为中心的LLM适配"的新范式，通过设计两个profile-conditioned算子（写端和读端）并联合训练，解决不同LLM之间内存迁移和适配的问题，使上游模型写入的内存能有效激活下游模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统以LLM为中心，内存操作针对特定骨干模型设计。但用户在实际使用中经常切换不同LLM（如用Claude编码、用GPT写作，或在同一任务中路由不同步骤到不同模型），导致一个模型写入的内存需要被另一个模型消费。上游内存如何有效适配并激活下游LLM是一个关键但未被充分探索的问题。

Method: 设计两个profile-conditioned算子（写端和读端）并联合训练；提出minimum-gain sampling curriculum优先训练服务最少的LLM；设计performance-gap reward来比较与朴素内存基线的性能差距，以衡量算子的实际贡献。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultihopQA和MuSiQue数据集上，模型一致优于基线，并且在未见过的模型替换下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 从LLM-centric转向memory-centric的范式是解决跨LLM内存适配问题的有效方向，所提方法在多种多跳问答任务上表现出色且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Memory is the key component for transforming a stateless LLM into a persistent, evolving agent through experience accumulation, long-horizon planning, and continual self-improvement. Existing memory systems typically take the LLM as the center and design memory operations tailored to a specific backbone. In practice, however, users frequently switch between LLMs, for example using Claude for coding and GPT for writing across tasks, or routing different steps to different backbones within a single task for cost-effective trade-offs. As a result, memory written by one model often needs to be consumed by another. Making upstream memory effectively adapt to and activate downstream LLMs remains a critical yet underexplored problem. To bridge this gap, we shift the perspective from LLM-centric memory design to \emph{memory-centric LLM adaptation}. Specifically, we approach the above upstream-downstream memory adaptation problem from both the write and read sides, and design two profile-conditioned operators that are jointly trained to optimize how memory is stored and presented for better task completion. To ensure the learned operators generalize across a broad set of LLMs, we propose a minimum-gain sampling curriculum that prioritizes the least-served LLMs during training. To better measure the operators' actual contribution rather than the LLM's own capability, we design a performance-gap reward that compares against a naive memory baseline. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, and MuSiQue demonstrate that our model consistently outperforms baselines and remains robust under unseen-model replacement.

</details>


### [81] [Attention at the Theoretical Minimum: A Mathematics of Arrays Framework for Memory-Optimal Transformer Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07713)
*Lenore Mullin,Gaetan Hains*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用数组数学（MoA）框架对注意力机制进行代数重构，推导出去除了所有中间数组的指称范式（DNF），将数据移动从O(n²)降至O(n)，无需经验调优即可实现内存最小化，预测可获得2-100倍加速和2-50倍能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Transformer中注意力机制的二次方内存流量是实际瓶颈，而传统仅关注FLOPs的分析严重误导了对瓶颈的理解，亟需一种代数化的、形式化的方法从根本上消除不必要的中间数组和数据移动。

Method: 基于数组数学（MoA）框架对缩放点积注意力及其数值稳定的softmax进行代数重构，推导出指称范式（DNF），通过代数构造而非经验调优消除所有中间数组（包括转置键缓冲区和所有softmax临时变量）。

Result: DNF实现了O(n·dk + n·dv)的数据移动，对比标准实现的O(n² + n·dk + n·dv)。预测性能模型显示2-100倍加速和2-50倍能耗降低，优势在超大规模下进一步扩大。数值验证使用全双精度浮点数与PyTorch进行比对。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一条从Python规范到操作范式再到硬件映射的形式化验证流水线，提供性能可移植的AI内核，直接服务于DARPA边缘部署和DOE超算优先级需求。

Abstract: The attention mechanism is the dominant computational bottleneck in modern transformer-based AI. Its standard implementation incurs quadratic memory traffic in the sequence length~$n$, and DRAM accesses cost 100--1000$\times$ more energy than arithmetic operations on contemporary hardware, so any analysis focused solely on FLOP counts fundamentally mischaracterises the bottleneck.
  We present a Mathematics of Arrays (MoA) reformulation of scaled dot-product attention and its numerically stable softmax, deriving a Denotational Normal Form (DNF) that eliminates all intermediate arrays -- including the implicit transposed-key buffer and every softmax temporary -- by algebraic construction rather than empirical tuning. The DNF achieves $O(n_{dk} + n{_{dv}})$ data movement versus $O(n^2 + n_{dk} + n_{dv})$ for the standard implementation, where $n$ is the sequence length, $dk$ is the key dimensionality and $dv$ the value dimensionality, and is verified numerically against PyTorch at full double-precision floating-point on concrete inputs.
  Unlike hardware-specific accelerators or empirical tiling schemes such as FlashAttention, MoA simultaneously provides array fusion, shape-transformation correctness, and predictive cost models from a single algebraic framework. Memory minimality is a theorem established before any code is written. A predictive performance model projects $2$--$100\times$ speedup and $2$--$50\times$ energy reduction, with the advantage widening at exascale. The derivation establishes a formally verified pipeline from Python specification through (ONF) Operational Normal Form, and dimension-lifted hardware mapping, providing performance-portable AI kernels of direct relevance to DARPA edge-deployment and DOE exascale priorities.

</details>


### [82] [Cutting LLM Evaluation Costs with SySRs: A Bandit Algorithm that Provably Exploits Model Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07726)
*Zifan Lyu,Chahine Nejma,Tobias Wegel,Fanny Yang,Florian E. Dorner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Synchronized Successive Rejects (SySR)算法，通过配对比较增强经典Successive Rejects方法，用于高效识别最佳大语言模型，在15个标准基准测试上优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上评估LLM需要对每个模型在所有测试查询上逐一评估，效率低下。实践中若某模型明显较差，精确评估其性能是浪费的。因此需要自适应分配评估预算来降低评估成本。

Method: 提出SySR算法，在经典Successive Rejects算法基础上引入配对比较机制，利用不同模型对相同提示的响应相似性。该方法无超参数，且性能保证随模型间相似度提高而增强。

Result: 在15个标准基准测试中，SySR在所有基线的平均错误率和可靠识别最佳模型的最坏情况预算方面均表现最优。

Conclusion: SySR是一种高效、无超参数的最佳模型识别算法，有效利用模型相似性提升识别效率，在LLM评估场景中显著降低成本。

Abstract: Large Language Models are typically benchmarked by evaluating every model on every test query. For practitioners seeking the best model to deploy, this is often wasteful: if a model clearly performs worse than others, there is no need to precisely estimate its performance. Best-arm identification algorithms can be naturally applied to drastically reduce costs by adaptively allocating evaluation budget. Further, language models often respond similarly to the same prompt-a property previous work has tried to leverage with mixed success. We propose Synchronized Successive Rejects (SySRs), augmenting the classical Successive Rejects algorithm with paired comparisons. Unlike prior attempts to leverage model similarity in best-model identification, our approach is hyperparameter-free and enjoys performance guarantees that improve with the degree of similarity between evaluated models. Empirically, our method outperforms all baselines in terms of average error rate across 15 standard benchmarks, and in terms of worst-case budget for reliably identifying the best model.

</details>


### [83] [Still: Amortized KV Cache Compaction in a Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07878)
*Charles O'Neill,Alex Sandomirsky,Harry Partridge,Mudith Jayasekara,Max Kirkby*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Still，一种轻量级的每层Perceiver压缩器，在单次前向传播中生成紧凑的KV缓存，支持8×到200×压缩比和8k到128k上下文长度，在长上下文基准测试中超越最强基线8-22分，并支持迭代式长程上下文压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程语言模型部署中KV缓存是内存瓶颈，现有压缩方法（选择法轻量但受限于子集，合成法表达力强但依赖逐上下文优化）无法同时满足轻量、表达力强和跨轨迹可复用的需求。

Method: Still方法：针对冻结的基础模型，训练一个轻量级的每层Perceiver网络，在单次前向传播中直接生成紧凑的键和值，无需逐上下文优化，且支持迭代式应用以处理超长上下文。

Result: 在Qwen和Gemma模型上，Still在8×到200×压缩比和8k到128k上下文范围内占据速度-质量前沿；在RULER长上下文基准上超越最强基线8-22分；在LongBench总结任务中优于KV-Distill。

Conclusion: 摊销（amortization）使长上下文缓存压缩变得可行，而合成方法使压缩状态在极端压缩率下仍然有用，证明了单次前向传播压缩器在实际部署中的有效性。

Abstract: The KV cache is the memory bottleneck of long-horizon language model deployment. Practically, a deployable compactor must be lightweight enough to call during inference, expressive enough to preserve context under constraint, and reusable across a trajectory. Existing compaction methods satisfy only part of this requirement: selection methods are lightweight but subset-bound, while synthesis methods are expressive but rely on per-context optimization. Here we introduce Still, a small per-layer Perceiver trained once against a frozen base model that produces compact keys and values in a single forward pass. On Qwen and Gemma models, Still occupies the favorable side of the speed--quality frontier across compression ratios from $8\times$ to $200\times$ and context lengths from $8$k to $128$k. On the long-context RULER grid, Still exceeds the strongest baseline by 8--22 points. The same compact cache also supports free-form summarization, preserving most of the full-context gain on HELMET and winning a pairwise LongBench summarization comparison against KV-Distill. Because compaction is a forward pass, Still can be applied iteratively, entering a long-horizon regime unavailable to per-context methods. We show that amortization makes long-context cache compaction tractable, and synthesis makes its compact state useful at extreme compression.

</details>


### [84] [Breaking the Bubble: Asynchronous Pipeline Parallel Training with Bounded Weight Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07881)
*Itay Elam,Eliron Rahimi,Avi Mendelson,Chaim Baskin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACI是一种无气泡的异步流水线并行方法，通过局部梯度累积作为版本控制机制来限制前向/后向版本漂移，无需权重暂存、预测或全局同步，在GPT风格语言模型预训练中匹配同步1F1B-flush的稳定性和最终困惑度，同时实现完全流水线吞吐量，训练时间加速比达1.69倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流水线并行调度需要在吞吐量、内存和优化一致性之间权衡：同步流水线存在气泡，异步流水线虽消除气泡但引入权重版本不匹配问题，通常需要权重暂存、预测或纠正机制，增加了复杂性和开销。

Method: PACI采用局部梯度积累作为版本控制机制，通过减缓参数版本演变相对于流水线延迟的速度，限制任何微批次跨越的优化器更新次数，从而在有界不一致下实现无气泡异步流水线训练。

Result: 在GPT风格语言模型预训练中，PACI匹配了同步1F1B-flush的稳定性和最终困惑度，保持相同峰值内存占用，实现完全流水线吞吐量，训练时间加速比最高达1.69倍。

Conclusion: 前向/后向不一致性无需被消除，当被显式界定时，可以安全地换取显著效率提升。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism is essential for training large neural networks, but existing schedules trade off throughput, memory, and optimization consistency. Synchronous pipelines preserve forward/backward weight consistency but suffer from bubbles; asynchronous pipelines remove bubbles but introduce weight-version mismatch, typically requiring weight stashing, prediction, or correction mechanisms. We introduce PACI (Pipeline Asynchronous training with Controlled Inconsistency), a bubble-free asynchronous pipeline method that bounds forward/backward version drift without weight stashing, prediction, additional parameter copies, or global synchronization. The key idea is to use local gradient accumulation as a version-control mechanism: by slowing parameter-version evolution relative to pipeline delay, PACI limits the number of optimizer updates crossed by any micro-batch while preserving steady-state utilization. In GPT-style language-model pretraining, PACI matches the stability and final perplexity of synchronous 1F1B-flush, retains the same peak memory footprint, achieves fully utilized pipeline throughput, and improves training time-to-accuracy by up to $1.69\times$ over the fastest flush baseline. These results show that forward/backward inconsistency need not be eliminated: when explicitly bounded, it can be safely traded for substantial efficiency gains.

</details>


### [85] [Semantic Quorum Assurance: Collective Certification for Non-Deterministic AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08021)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义法定确认（SQA）机制，通过多样化只读验证器面板和风险自适应法定谓词，对LLM代理生成的云基础设施变更进行安全评估，将不安全批准率从18.5%降至0.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在自主云操作中可能生成语法有效、静态授权但操作不安全的变更（如修改IAM策略、开放防火墙等），现有分布式共识协议无法评估提议意图的安全性。

Method: 将提案表示为声明性执行合约绑定密码学证据链，路由到多样化只读沙箱验证器面板，通过风险自适应法定谓词聚合判断，强制模型和原型多样性，依据校准置信度调整权重，尊重原型特定否决权，通过主权执行门执行批准的提案。

Result: 在500个基础设施启发的变更场景中，SQA将不安全批准率从单代理验证的18.5%降至0.3%，中位验证延迟为1.45-4.12秒。

Conclusion: SQA作为控制平面原语，有效解决了非确定性代理基础设施的安全治理问题，显著提升了云基础设施变更的安全性。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents are integrated into autonomous cloud operations, distributed systems face a semantic reliability problem: proposer agents can generate production mutations, such as modifying IAM policies, opening firewall security groups, or executing data exports, that are syntactically valid and statically authorized but operationally unsafe. Classical distributed consensus protocols replicate deterministic state transitions but do not evaluate the safety of the proposed intent. To address this gap, we introduce Semantic Quorum Assurance (SQA), a control-plane primitive for governing non-deterministic agentic infrastructure. SQA represents proposals as declarative execution contracts bound to cryptographic evidence chains and routes them to a diverse panel of read-only, sandboxed validator agents. SQA aggregates their judgments under a risk-adaptive quorum predicate that enforces model and archetype diversity, adjusts weights based on calibrated assurance scores, and respects archetype-specific vetoes. Admitted proposals execute only through a sovereign execution gate. We instantiate SQA in a cloud-native control plane and formalize a correlated cognitive failure model for non-deterministic validators. On 500 infrastructure-inspired mutation scenarios, with safety results reported on held-out safe/unsafe trials excluding ambiguous scenarios, SQA reduces unsafe approval from 18.5% for single-agent validation to 0.3% while adding median validation latency of 1.45--4.12 seconds across the studied risk buckets.

</details>


### [86] [LogNEO: A GPT-Neo Reinforcement Learning Framework for Accurate Real-Time Log Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08153)
*David Eje,Tanmay Sharma,Khush Patel,Manuel Mazzara,Leonard Johard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LogNEO是一个基于GPT-Neo (1.3B参数)的日志异常检测器，使用部分信用指数衰减位置感知奖励方案和PPO进行微调，在HDFS、BGL和Thunderbird基准上分别达到0.927、0.913和0.984的F1分数，并在生产微服务部署中实现45ms端到端延迟和15,000事件/秒的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模系统日志中的异常检测对现代计算基础设施的可靠性和安全性至关重要，现有方法在准确性和效率上仍有改进空间。

Method: 基于GPT-Neo (1.3B参数)模型，采用部分信用指数衰减位置感知奖励方案结合交叉熵正则化，通过PPO进行微调。位置感知奖励显式建模预测难度：早期位置正确预测获更高奖励，后期位置错误预测受更强惩罚。

Result: 在HDFS、BGL和Thunderbird基准上F1分数分别为0.927、0.913和0.984，召回率相比先前最优LogGPT提升最多6个百分点，同时保持相当的精确率。生产部署中45ms端到端延迟，吞吐量15,000事件/秒。

Conclusion: LogNEO在日志异常检测任务上达到了新的最优性能，并且具有实际生产部署的可行性。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in large-scale system logs is critical for the reliability and security of modern computing infrastructure. We present LogNEO, a log anomaly detector built on EleutherAI's GPT-Neo (1.3B parameters) and fine-tuned with a novel partial-credit, exponentially decaying position-aware reward scheme combined with cross-entropy regularisation via Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO). The position-aware reward explicitly models prediction difficulty: early positions receive higher rewards for correct predictions, while later positions incur stronger penalties for errors. LogNEO attains F1-scores of 0.927, 0.913, and 0.984 on the HDFS, BGL, and Thunderbird benchmarks, improving recall by up to 6 percentage points over the prior state-of-the-art LogGPT while maintaining comparable precision. A production microservice deployment over Apache Kafka, Redis, and TensorRT-accelerated inference demonstrates 45 ms end-to-end latency at 15,000 events per second.

</details>


### [87] [AttentionCap: Transformer Based Capacitance Matrix Learning Toward Full-Chip Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08161)
*Jiechen Huang,Hector R. Rodriguez,Dingcheng Yang,Zuochang Ye,Yibo Lin,Wenjian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AttentionCap，一种基于Transformer的电容矩阵学习方法，通过Gram表示框架、对称注意力输出层和归一化拉普拉斯损失，结合工艺节点嵌入实现多节点学习，在多层多节点设置下超越CNN基线，实现4.6×/5.7×更低误差和192×更快推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着先进工艺节点下基于规则的模式匹配电容提取精度难以维持，现有MLP和CNN方法局限于固定金属层组合和特定工艺节点，实用性受限。

Method: 提出AttentionCap Transformer模型，包含Gram表示框架、物理对齐的对称注意力输出层、归一化拉普拉斯损失以及工艺节点嵌入，支持多节点学习。

Result: 在未见过的真实设计上达到0.67%/3.99%自/耦合电容误差，相比CNN-Cap基线降低4.6×/5.7×误差，实现192×更快的推理速度；预训练模型仅需5K样本和4K微调步骤即可迁移到未见节点。

Conclusion: AttentionCap在未见真实设计上具有足够精度和强迁移性，为现代EDA工作流提供了高实用价值。

Abstract: As capacitance extraction accuracy of rule-based pattern matching becomes difficult to sustain at advanced nodes, a growing trend emerges to develop deep-learning-based 2D capacitance models. However, existing MLP- and CNN-based methods constrain their input to fixed metal-layer combinations in a specific process node, limiting their usability in practice. Recognizing the inherent similarity between capacitance matrix and the prevailing attention mechanism, we propose AttentionCap, a customized Transformer for capacitance matrix learning, with a Gram representation framework, a physics-aligned symmetric-attention output layer, and a novel normalized Laplacian loss. We also introduce a process-node embedding to enable multi-node learning. Trained on synthetic data, AttentionCap attains 0.67\%/3.99\% self/coupling-capacitance error on unseen real designs under a multi-layer and multi-node setting, surpassing the CNN-Cap baseline with 4.6$\times$/5.7$\times$ lower self/coupling error and 192$\times$ faster inference speed. A pretrained AttentionCap accurately transfers to an unseen node with only 5K samples and 4K finetuning steps. With sufficient accuracy on unseen real designs and strong transferability to new process nodes, AttentionCap offers highly practical value for modern EDA workflows. Code and data are available at https://github.com/THU-numbda/AttentionCap.

</details>


### [88] [Explaining Data Mixing Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08167)
*Rui Dai,Shuran Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一理论框架解释多领域数据混合的缩放定律，基于能力竞争和噪声减少两个关键因素，在更少参数下实现更优的损失拟合和跨规模外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅建立了多领域数据混合的实证缩放定律，缺乏背后的理论基础，作者希望从理论层面统一解释数据混合的内在机制。

Method: 将标准神经缩放定律（如Kaplan和Chinchilla）的理论视角扩展到多领域场景，基于领域在基础技能上重叠、在专门技能上分化的分布假设，识别出容量竞争（Capacity Competition）和噪声减少（Noise Reduction）两个关键因素来建模领域损失。

Result: 框架以更低的平均相对误差拟合损失曲面，能识别出更高性能的训练混合比例，且能用小规模拟合的参数预测大规模下高效的数据混合方案，同时使用的参数显著少于经验定律。

Conclusion: 该理论框架有效解释了数据混合缩放定律的底层机制，具有更好的拟合精度和跨规模外推能力，且参数效率更高。

Abstract: Recent research has established empirical scaling laws to predict model performance on multi-domain data mixtures. However, a theoretical understanding of these model loss behaviors remains absent. In this work, we propose a unified framework to explain the underlying mechanics of data mixing. Our approach extends theoretical perspectives originally developed for standard neural scaling laws (e.g., Kaplan and Chinchilla) to the multi-domain setting. Based on the distributional assumption that domains overlap on fundamental skills while diverging on specialized skills, we identify two key factors that govern the domain losses of models trained on different data mixtures: \textit{Capacity Competition}, where the allocation of finite model capacity couples domain losses globally, and \textit{Noise Reduction}, where optimal weights shift toward harder-to-learn domains to minimize overall noise. Empirical evaluations show that our framework outperforms existing baselines by fitting the loss landscape with a lower Mean Relative Error and identifying higher-performing training mixtures. Most importantly, our model successfully extrapolates across scales, predicting highly effective mixtures for large, unseen scales using parameters fitted on smaller ones. In addition, our model achieves these results using significantly fewer parameters compared to previous empirical laws. Our code is available at https://github.com/meiqwq/Explaining-Data-Mixing-Scaling-Laws.

</details>


### [89] [Causal Agent Replay: Counterfactual Attribution for LLM-Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08275)
*Jaineet Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Causal Agent Replay (CAR)，通过将LLM智能体运行建模为结构因果模型并进行干预操作（do-operation），结合对比估计器和蒙特卡洛Shapley估计器，精确定位导致失败的关键步骤，解决了现有工具只能回答"发生了什么"和"是否通过"但无法定位根因的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体工具只能回答"发生了什么"（可观测性）或"是否通过"（评估），但无法定位哪个步骤导致了失败；且简单的启发式方法（如归因于执行有害行为的步骤）是错误的，LLM裁判归因效果也很差（Who&When基准上步骤级准确率仅约14%）。

Method: 将智能体运行建模为结构因果模型，对特定步骤施加do操作后在其随机策略下重新执行轨迹，通过对比估计器衡量结果分布的变化；定义了干预代数、解决随机前向执行中混杂问题的承诺点规则，以及预算约束的蒙特卡洛Shapley估计器来跨交互步骤分配归因分数。

Result: 在合成结构因果模型上验证：对比估计器能准确恢复关键步骤，Shapley估计器能恢复两步交互（0.44, 0.45, ~0；效率总和0.909 vs 解析值0.91）。

Conclusion: CAR通过因果干预而非相关性归因，提供了更可靠的智能体失败根因定位方法，开源且可运行在托管或免费本地模型上。

Abstract: When an LLM agent fails -- issues a refund it should not have, calls the wrong tool, leaks data -- existing tooling answers what happened (observability) or whether it passed (evaluation), but not which step caused the failure. The obvious heuristics are wrong: the step that executes the harmful action is usually not the step that decided on it, and LLM-judge attribution is correlational and unreliable (state-of-the-art step-level accuracy on the Who&When benchmark is about 14%). We present Causal Agent Replay (CAR), which answers the question by intervention: it models an agent run as a structural causal model, applies a do-operation to a step, and re-executes the trajectory forward under the same stochastic policy, measuring the shift in the outcome distribution. We define an intervention algebra over agent steps, a single-step contrastive estimator whose point-of-commitment rule resolves a confound specific to stochastic run-forward, and a budget-bounded Monte-Carlo Shapley estimator that splits credit across interacting steps. Every effect is reported with confidence intervals. We validate against synthetic structural causal models with planted ground truth: the contrastive estimator recovers the pivotal step, and Shapley recovers a two-step interaction (0.44, 0.45, ~0; efficiency sum 0.909 versus the analytic 0.91). CAR is open source and runs on hosted or free local models.

</details>


### [90] [STAR-KV: Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Soft Thresholding for Adaptive Rank Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08382)
*Priyansh Bhatnagar,Ashkan Moradifirouzabadi,Se-Hyun Yang,SeungJae Lee,Jungwook Choi,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAR-KV提出了一种自适应低秩KV缓存压缩框架，通过可微阈值机制实现细粒度秩选择，结合混合分解策略和低秩感知混合精度量化，在多种LLM上达到75%压缩率，结合量化后整体KV缓存缩减达20倍，并利用Triton GPU内核实现6.9倍注意力模块加速和3.1倍端到端吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩投影方法依赖固定或启发式秩选择，难以在激进压缩下保持低精度损失，需要更灵活的自适应秩选择机制。

Method: 1) 可微阈值机制实现注意力头和块级别的优化秩选择；2) 根据键/值投影敏感度采用不同低秩分解的混合策略；3) 利用数据统计进行近无损低比特量化的低秩感知混合精度量化。

Result: 高达75% KV缓存压缩，结合量化后整体缩减20倍；Triton GPU内核带来6.9倍注意力模块加速和3.1倍端到端生成吞吐提升。

Conclusion: STAR-KV在KV缓存压缩上显著优于现有方法，实现了高效且精度友好的压缩方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Low-rank projection has emerged as a promising approach for compressing the KV cache by exploiting hidden-dimension redundancy. However, prior methods rely on fixed or heuristic rank selection and struggle to achieve aggressive compression with minimal accuracy degradation. We propose STAR-KV, an adaptive low-rank KV cache compression framework with fine-grained rank control. STAR-KV encompasses 1) a differentiable thresholding mechanism that enables optimal rank selection at both attention-head and block levels, 2) a hybrid decomposition strategy that applies different low-rank factorizations according to the sensitivity of key and value projections, and 3) a low-rank-aware mixed precision quantization that leverages data statistics for near lossless low-bit quantization. Evaluated across multiple LLMs and benchmarks, STAR-KV achieves up to 75% KV cache compression and up to 20x overall KV cache reduction when combined with quantization. Enabled by custom Triton-based GPU kernels, STAR-KV delivers up to 6.9x speedup for the attention module and 3.1x end-to-end generation throughput. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/PriyanshBhatnagar/STAR-KV.

</details>


### [91] [The Spectral Dynamics and Noise Geometry of Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08388)
*Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Mahmoud Abdelmoneum,Tomaso Poggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Muon优化器（用梯度矩阵的极因子UV^T替代SVD梯度）的优化偏差，证明其倾向于使更新谱平坦化，并在理论上和实验上分析了这种偏差在不同场景下的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器已在实践中被使用，但其内部工作原理和优化偏差尚未被充分理解，本文旨在从理论上揭示极更新操作带来的谱平坦化偏向。

Method: 通过理论分析（在显式对齐假设下证明极更新是熵最大化选择、推导连续时间Muon的奇异值动力学）、矩阵感知实验和小规模NanoGPT/ViT训练对比来研究Muon的优化偏差。

Result: Muon的极更新使谱平坦化，在NanoGPT预训练中比AdamW验证损失更低、学习率平台更宽，但在小ViT实验中效果相反。

Conclusion: Muon并非普遍优于其他优化器，其平坦谱偏向在需要保持多个谱方向活跃时有益，但存在情景依赖性。

Abstract: Muon replaces a matrix gradient $G=UΣV^\top$ by its polar factor $UV^\top$. This keeps the singular directions selected by the gradient, but makes the update spectrum flat. We study the optimization bias created by this operation. Under explicit alignment assumptions, we prove that the polar update is the one-step entropy-maximizing choice among bounded updates that use the gradient singular directions and do not adapt to the current weight spectrum. In an underdetermined regression model, we derive exact singular-value dynamics for continuous-time Muon and identify a measurement-dependent condition under which the normalized spectrum moves toward equal nonzero singular values. This geometry also rules out a common low-rank interpretation: at fixed Frobenius norm, Muon's distinguished state has a flat spectrum, whereas nuclear-norm minimization favors spectral concentration. Controlled matrix-sensing experiments separate the effect from simple gradient rescaling, show that norm-matched gradient descent does not reproduce Muon, and recover the predicted flattening trend across broad ablations. In small NanoGPT pretraining, Muon preserves stable rank, has a broad learning-rate plateau, and improves validation loss relative to AdamW; in a matched small-ViT control, the ranking reverses. The resulting picture is regime-dependent: Muon is not universally superior, but its flat-spectrum bias can help when many spectral directions need to remain active.

</details>


### [92] [Sparrow: Sparse Rollout for Stable and Efficient Long-context RL of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08446)
*Yang Zhou,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Zhaofeng Sun,Zhuoming Chen,Souvik Kundu,Saket Dingliwal,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Aram Galstyan,Haizhong Zheng,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了动态稀疏注意力调度方法，通过在RLVR训练中保持token级策略差异的尾部统计量稳定，在不引发训练崩溃的前提下实现2.0-2.4x的rollout加速，并引入DistillSparse（基于LoRA的蒸馏）进一步加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR训练中长上下文rollout生成的计算成本极高，稀疏注意力虽可加速但存在稳定性与效率的权衡：过于激进的稀疏会导致训练崩溃，过于保守则加速不足。

Method: 观察到稀疏rollout崩溃并非源于所有token的一致退化，而是由稀疏到密集的策略差异尾部决定。提出在生成过程中保持该尾部统计量恒定的动态稀疏调度，并通过成本模型寻找在该阈值下最大化加速比的稀疏方案。进一步提出DistillSparse，利用轻量LoRA蒸馏使更激进的稀疏达到相同阈值。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B上分别实现2.2x、2.4x、2.0x的rollout加速；阈值可泛化到Qwen3-14B和编码领域；DistillSparse可进一步提升加速比。

Conclusion: 通过保持稀疏到密集策略差异的尾部阈值，可以在不牺牲训练稳定性的前提下大幅加速RLVR的rollout生成，DistillSparse可进一步提升效率。

Abstract: Despite being powerful, reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) induces extremely long COT, making it computationally expensive. Since RLVR per-step cost is dominated by long-context rollout generation, sparse attention offers a promising way to accelerate dense rollout. However, sparse rollouts require a delicate stability-efficiency tradeoff: overly aggressive sparsity causes collapse, while overly lenient sparsity gives insufficient speedup. In this work, we study this tradeoff through sparse-to-dense actor-policy mismatch. We first observe that sparse rollout collapse is not driven by uniform degradation across tokens: most sparse tokens align perfectly with dense even under aggressive sparsity. Motivated by this, we hypothesize that sparse rollout training remains stable if the lower tail of per-token actor-policy mismatch stays above a critical threshold throughout the trajectory. We introduce a dynamic sparsity schedule that keeps this tail statistic constant during generation and validate our hypothesis. Across Qwen3 thinking-family models, keeping the tail mismatch statistic near a consistent threshold generally enables stable training. We then use a cost model to find the sparsity schedule for maximum speedup under this mismatch threshold, achieving 2.2x, 2.4x, and 2.0x rollout speedups when training Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B. Empirically, we show the thresholds generalize to a larger model (Qwen3-14B) and another RL domain (coding). Finally, our analysis naturally motivates DistillSparse: lightweight LoRA-based distillation on sparse rollout lets more aggressive sparsity reach the same sparse-to-dense mismatch threshold, yielding higher speedup.

</details>


### [93] [EinSort: Sorting is All We Need for Tensorizing LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08565)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Jing Liu,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应张量化方法，通过索引排序发现目标张量中的内在低秩结构，用于压缩大型神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型基础模型规模巨大且权重分布非结构化，难以识别隐含的低秩结构，需要一种自动发现低秩结构的方法来压缩模型。

Method: 提出自适应张量化方法，通过优化索引排序来发现张量中的内在低秩结构，从而实现更有效的张量网络表示。

Result: 在权重和KV缓存压缩任务上，相比基线方法实现了更好的重建质量。

Conclusion: 自适应张量化能有效发现大型模型中的低秩结构，提升压缩性能。

Abstract: Tensor networks provide efficient representations for compressing large neural networks. By carefully designing shapes and topologies, they can significantly reduce memory and computational costs. However, identifying implicit low-rank structures in large foundation models remains challenging due to their enormous scale and un-structured weight distributions. We propose an adaptive tensorization method that discovers inherent low-rank structure in a target tensor by index ordering. Experiments on weight and KV-cache compression demonstrate improved reconstruction quality compared to baselines.

</details>


### [94] [OrderDP: A Theoretically Guaranteed Lossless Dynamic Data Pruning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08574)
*Chenhan Jin,Shengze Xu,Qingsong Wang,Fan Jia,Dingshuo Chen,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OrderDP框架，通过先随机采样再选择top-q样本的方式实现无偏数据剪枝，在保证理论收敛性和泛化性能的同时减少40%以上训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据剪枝方法倾向于选择高信息量样本，导致梯度估计有偏，且这种偏差对最终性能的影响尚不明确。OrderDP旨在解决有偏梯度估计问题，实现稳定、无偏、近无损的训练加速。

Method: OrderDP是一个即插即用框架：先随机选择子集，再从该子集中选出top-q样本；通过代理损失（surrogate loss）建立无偏性保证，并提供收敛性和泛化性的理论分析。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1K上，OrderDP相比基线方法取得了有竞争力的精度、稳定的收敛和精确的控制，训练成本降低超过40%。

Conclusion: OrderDP是一种鲁棒且易于适配的数据高效学习工具，兼顾了强性能和计算效率。

Abstract: Data pruning (DP), as an oft-stated strategy to alleviate heavy training burdens, reduces the volume of training samples according to a well-defined pruning method while striving for near-lossless performance. However, existing approaches, which commonly select highly informative samples, can lead to biased gradient estimation compared to full-dataset training. Furthermore, the analysis of this bias and its impact on final performance remains ambiguous. To address these challenges, we propose OrderDP, a plug-and-play framework that aims to obtain stable, unbiased, and near-lossless training acceleration with theoretical guarantees. Specifically, OrderDP first randomly selects a subset and then chooses the top-$q$ samples, where unbiasedness is established with respect to a surrogate loss. This ensures that OrderDP conducts unbiased training in terms of the surrogate objective. We further establish convergence and generalization analyses, elucidating how OrderDP affects optimal performance and enables well-controlled acceleration while ensuring guaranteed final performance. Empirically, we evaluate OrderDP against comprehensive baselines on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-1K, demonstrating competitive accuracy, stable convergence, and exact control -- all with a simpler design and faster runtime, while reducing training cost by over 40%. Delivering both strong performance and computational efficiency, our method serves as a robust and easily adaptable tool for data-efficient learning. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/shengze-xu/OrderDP.

</details>


### [95] [How Much Capacity Does EEG Denoising Need? Ultra-Compact Networks reveal Benchmark Saturation and Metric-Utility Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08594)
*Jasmeet Singh Bindra,Siddharth Panwar,Shubhajit Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验发现，EEG去噪模型在3-6.5K参数时重构性能即饱和，远低于当前数千万参数的规模；且重构指标无法预测下游BCI任务效用，最优去噪模型反而显著降低了CSP+LDA分类准确率。超紧凑模型（33-46KB，1.27-2.61M FLOPs）适合边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG深度学习去噪研究从数万参数扩展到数千万参数，但从未将模型容量作为独立实验变量进行隔离测试，也未曾检验重构指标是否能预测下游神经信号的实际可用性。本文旨在填补这两个空白。

Method: 固定架构、损失函数、数据划分和训练方案，仅改变通道宽度（1.05K到40.26K参数），在最小深度可分离卷积U-Net上实验。在EEGDenoiseNet基准、跨数据集BCI迁移测试、控制基线重训练以及所有9个BCI Competition IV-2a受试者的五类解码器上进行下游运动想象分类评估。

Result: 重构性能在3-6.5K参数时饱和；8.46M参数基线与40.26K紧凑变体在EOG上效果相同（200倍参数差距无优势）；Patch-Transformer复现了相同的递减回报曲线。下游评估显示，重构优化的去噪显著降低了所有9个受试者的CSP+LDA分类精度（最佳去噪0.547 vs 噪声基线0.612）。

Conclusion: 标准EEG去噪基准在当前模型容量远未达到时已经饱和，重构指标无法预测BCI效用。超紧凑模型（33-46KB，1.27-2.61M FLOPs/段）适合边缘部署。研究呼吁进行容量控制的评估、更困难的任务感知基准以及强制性的下游验证。

Abstract: Deep learning EEG denoising architectures have scaled from tens of thousands to tens of millions of parameters, yet no prior study has isolated model capacity as the experimental variable or tested whether reconstruction metrics predict downstream neural-signal utility. We address both gaps by fixing architecture, loss, data split, and training recipe while sweeping only channel width from 1.05K to 40.26K parameters in a minimal depthwise-separable convolutional U-Net. Models were evaluated on the EEGDenoiseNet benchmark, cross-dataset BCI transfer tests, controlled baseline retraining, and downstream motor-imagery classification with five decoder families across all nine BCI Competition IV-2a subjects. Reconstruction performance saturated by 3-6.5K parameters, with post-elbow gains of at most 0.015 correlation coefficient per log10-parameter unit. An 8.46M-parameter baseline retrained under the same pipeline matched the 40.26K compact variant on EOG--a 200x parameter gap yielding no advantage--while a Patch-Transformer control reproduced the same diminishing-return shape. Downstream evaluation exposed a classifier-dependent metric-utility gap: reconstruction-optimized denoising significantly degraded CSP+LDA classification across all nine subjects and three artifact types (best denoised accuracy 0.547 vs. 0.612 noisy baseline; Bonferroni p=0.0488), persisting on naturally recorded trials (Delta=-0.047; BH-FDR q=0.0049). End-to-end neural decoders showed variable or neutral effects. Standard EEG denoising benchmarks are saturated far below current model capacity, and reconstruction metrics do not predict BCI utility. Ultra-compact models at 33-46 KB and 1.27-2.61M FLOPs/segment are practical for edge deployment. These findings argue for capacity-controlled evaluation, harder task-aware benchmarks, and mandatory downstream validation.

</details>


### [96] [Reformulate LLM Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Training under Black-box Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08779)
*Jiashun Liu,Runze Liu,Xu Wan,Jing Liang,Hongyao Tang,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Discrepancy-Constrained MDP (DCMDP)框架，通过约束训练-推理行为差异来解决强化学习中的训练-推理不一致问题，实现稳定的双目标优化，在8B和30B MoE模型上取得显著提升，并支持异构训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练常因训练与推理引擎/架构差异导致隐藏的不一致性问题，引发次优性能甚至训练崩溃。

Method: 提出DCMDP框架，将奖励最大化与训练-推理行为对齐约束耦合，并引入Lagrangian松弛机制动态调整两目标权重，在容忍区域内允许策略自由探索，超出边界时引导回正。

Result: 在Qwen-3-8B和Qwen-3-30B-A3B上显著提升性能，支持异构训练（高保真训练+低成本推理部署）。

Conclusion: DCMDP能有效解决RL训练-推理不一致问题，在保持探索效率的同时提升优化稳定性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a pivotal post-training paradigm, yet it frequently suffers from unpredictable sub-optimum performance or even training collapses. Recent findings attribute these failures to a hidden train-inference discrepancy (or mismatch), stemming from the disparate underlying engines and architecture. We find that the training policy can actively self-correct such a discrepancy when provided with an appropriate learning signal. Then, we further empirically identify a discrepancy tolerance region: within this region, aggressively narrowing the discrepancy can suppress policy exploration and reduce learning efficiency, whereas outside this region, reducing excessive discrepancy improves optimization consistency and raises the achievable local performance ceiling. According to such findings, we formulate this problem as a Discrepancy-Constrained Markov Decision Process (DCMDP), where reward maximization is coupled with a constraint that aligns training-Inference behavior, achieving stable dual-objective optimization. To adaptively balance performance improvement and discrepancy control, we introduce a Lagrangian relaxation mechanism that dynamically adjusts the relative weight of the two objectives according to the current degree of discrepancy violation. This enables stable dual-objective optimization: the policy is allowed to explore freely within the tolerance region, while being guided back when the discrepancy exceeds the safe boundary. Empirically, DCMDP significantly improves the performance of 8B dense model (Qwen-3-8b) and 30B Mixture-of-Expert model (Qwen-3-30bA3b), and enables a heterogeneous training paradigm, where LLMs can be optimized in high-fidelity training setup while being explicitly aligned for low-cost, resource-constrained inference deployment.

</details>


### [97] [sGPO: Trading Inference FLOPs for Training Efficiency in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08854)
*Shivchander Sudalairaj,Kai Xu,Akash Srivastava,Giorgio Giannone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出sGPO（排序组策略优化），通过少量推理计算作为查询难度的离线代理，自适应调整训练时的rollout预算分配，避免在已解决或不可解查询上浪费计算资源，实现总训练计算量降低3倍的同时保持或超越基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR训练对所有查询分配固定的rollout预算，未考虑查询难度差异，导致简单查询产生近零优势（策略已能解决），不可解查询产生无信号（策略从未解决），两者都浪费训练FLOPs且不贡献梯度。

Method: sGPO通过一次离线推理profiling：对每个查询在初始策略下生成小批量并行样本以获得模型感知的经验成功率；将训练rollout组大小设为成功率的倒数；同时驱动数据过滤（移除简单查询、子采样不可解查询）、自适应组大小分配和课程学习（从易到难排序查询）。

Result: sGPO在匹配或超越基线性能的同时，将总训练计算量降低3倍（包含前期的推理profiling成本）。

Conclusion: 用少量推理计算作为查询难度的离线代理，可以大幅减少训练中浪费的FLOPs，是一种更高效的计算资源分配策略。

Abstract: Standard Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) training allocates a fixed rollout budget to every query, without regard for what each query's difficulty means for the current policy. This leads to two symmetric failure modes: easy queries produce near-zero advantage because the policy already solves them, while unsolvable queries produce no signal because the policy never solves them. Both regimes waste training FLOPs without contributing to a learning gradient. We introduce sorted Group Policy Optimization (sGPO), a compute-efficient strategy that trades a small budget of inference FLOPs for a large reduction in wasted training FLOPs. The key insight is that cheap inference compute can serve as a single offline proxy for query difficulty. By generating a small batch of parallel samples per query under the initial policy, we obtain a model-aware empirical success rate. This motivates setting the training rollout group size to the inverse of this success rate, a practical rule that maximizes sample efficiency by extracting the most advantage per generated rollout. This single profiling pass simultaneously drives data filtering (removing trivial queries and sub-sampling unsolvable ones), adaptive group size allocation, and curriculum construction (scheduling queries from easy to hard). sGPO matches or exceeds baseline performance while reducing total training compute by a factor of three, with the upfront inference profiling cost included.

</details>


### [98] [C$^3$ache: Accelerating World Action Models with Cross Inference Chunk Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08962)
*Weisen Zhao,Lam Nguyen,Zhicong Lu,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: C$^3$ache是一种针对世界动作模型(WAM)的无训练推理加速方法，通过跨推理块缓存和复用去噪残差，在Fast-WAM骨干上实现高达2.5倍的总推理时间加速，且任务成功率几乎无损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM因视频建模目标需要多块推理且每块需昂贵去噪过程，现有加速方法仅考虑单块内去噪轨迹的冗余，忽略了块间冗余，导致推理效率低。

Method: 提出C$^3$ache，一种无训练方法，利用机器人执行平滑行为时相邻块在同一去噪步的残差高度相关这一特性，跨块缓存并复用残差计算。

Result: 在Fast-WAM骨干上的基准测试中，C$^3$ache实现最高2.5倍总端到端推理时间加速，任务成功率下降可忽略不计。

Conclusion: C$^3$ache有效利用了WAM推理中跨块的冗余，是一种简单高效的训练免费加速方案。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) generalize better than standard Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies to novel motions and environments, because a video-modeling objective lets them learn from abundant unlabeled video rather than scarce labeled robot demonstrations. This generalization is computationally expensive. To complete a task, a WAM runs over multiple inference chunks, and each chunk requires a costly denoising process. Existing acceleration methods reduce this cost by caching and reusing computation within a single chunk's denoising trajectory. Our empirical analysis reveals a substantial source of redundancy they overlook: redundancy across chunks. When a robot executes a smooth behavior, the residuals computed at a given denoising step are strongly correlated from one chunk to the next. We introduce C$^3$ache, a training-free method that caches and reuses these residuals across inference chunks at the same denoising step. Experiments on benchmarks with a Fast-WAM backbone show that C$^3$ache achieves up to a $2.5\times$ speedup in total wall-clock inference time, with negligible degradation in task success rate.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond FLOPs: Benchmarking Real Inference Acceleration of LLM Pruning under a GEMM-Centric Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09080)
*Haozhe Hu,Hao Wu,Anhao Zhao,Longwei Ding,Peiran Yin,Yunpu Ma,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GEMM维度的剪枝方法分类法，构建了统一基准框架，系统比较了各种LLM剪枝方法的加速-质量帕累托前沿，发现静态深度剪枝在大多数场景下是最强的帕累托最优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在多种LLM剪枝加速方法，但由于它们导致不同的执行行为，实际加速效果严重依赖硬件和内核实现，不同剪枝方法族的实际加速收益尚不明确。

Method: 提出以GEMM的M、N、K维度为核心的剪枝分类法，构建统一的基准测试框架，实现跨剪枝设计空间的一致性比较，系统表征加速-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 静态深度剪枝是最强的帕累托最优基线，在内存受限场景下最接近理论加速上限；预填充阶段帕累托前沿随质量损失程度从静态深度逐渐过渡到动态深度再到静态宽度剪枝。

Conclusion: 建立了首个剪枝加速LLM实际限制的统一视角，为未来剪枝研究提供指导。

Abstract: Pruning has emerged as a dominant paradigm for accelerating large language model (LLM) inference, spanning a broad spectrum of methods that remove computation across tokens, layers, heads, dimensions, and attention patterns. Despite sharing the same objective, these pruning approaches induce fundamentally different execution behaviors, causing realized speedups to depend heavily on hardware and kernel implementations. Consequently, the practical acceleration benefits of different pruning families remain poorly understood. In this work, we introduce a GEMM-centric taxonomy that reorganizes existing pruning methods according to the logical \textbf{M}, \textbf{N}, and \textbf{K} dimensions of general matrix multiplication (GEMM). Leveraging this abstraction, we build a unified benchmarking framework that enables implementation-consistent comparison across the pruning design space and systematically characterizes the acceleration--quality Pareto frontier. Our results show that static depth pruning remains the strongest Pareto-optimal baseline and stays closest to its theoretical acceleration upper bound in memory-bounded scenarios. During prefill, the frontier transitions from static depth at low quality loss (0\%--4\%), to dynamic depth at moderate loss (5\%--16\%), and finally to static width pruning at higher loss levels (17\%--26\%). These findings establish the first unified view of the practical limits of pruning-based LLM acceleration and provide guidance for future pruning research.\footnote{Code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning/tree/main/PruningInferSim}

</details>


### [100] [Claw-R1: A Step-Level Data Middleware System for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09138)
*Daoyu Wang,Mingyue Cheng,Qingchuan Li,Shuo Yu,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Claw-R1是一个面向智能体强化学习的交互式步骤级数据中间件系统，连接异构智能体运行时与RL训练后端，通过Gateway Server和Data Pool管理智能体-环境交互的全数据生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注策略优化算法和训练框架，但忽视了智能体-环境交互从数据生产到训练消费的完整数据生命周期管理。

Method: 提出Claw-R1系统，包含Gateway Server（通过统一LLM API入口捕获多轮交互步骤）和Data Pool（将交互组织为包含prompt ID、response ID、奖励等元数据的步骤级记录），支持用户交互式检查轨迹、按质量和就绪程度筛选数据、配置训练批次。

Result: Claw-R1将智能体交互轨迹视为受管理的数据资产而非临时运行时日志，支持实时轨迹检查、步骤级状态/动作/奖励查看、数据筛选和训练批次配置。

Conclusion: 希望通过该演示鼓励社区认识到数据管理在智能体强化学习中的重要性。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has become an important post-training paradigm for turning LLMs from static chatbots into interactive agents, giving rise to representative applications such as OpenClaw. Existing work mainly focuses on policy optimization algorithms and training frameworks, but pays less attention to the full data lifecycle of agent-environment interactions, from data production to training consumption. To bridge this gap, we present Claw-R1, an interactive step-level data middleware system for agentic RL. Claw-R1 connects heterogeneous agent runtimes with RL training backends through two core components: a Gateway Server and a Data Pool. The Gateway Server captures multi-turn interaction steps through a unified LLM API entry point, while the Data Pool organizes them into step-level records consisting of prompt IDs, response IDs, rewards and other metadata. In our demo, users can interactively inspect live trajectories, examine the state, action, and reward of each step, curate data by quality and readiness, and configure training-ready batches for different downstream RL algorithms. Overall, Claw-R1 treats agent interaction traces as managed data assets rather than temporary runtime logs. Through this demonstration, we hope to encourage the community to recognize the importance of data management in agentic RL. Our code is available at https://github.com/AgentR1/Claw-R1 and the demonstration video can be found at link https://youtu.be/Pw47dAOw6B0.

</details>


### [101] [The Hidden Bias of Process Reward Models:PRISM for Rewarding the Right Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09078)
*Aakriti Agrawal,Souradip Chakraborty,Armin Saghafian,Nihal Sharma,Rizal Fathony,Nam H Nguyen,C. Bayan Bruss,Amrit Singh Bedi,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现过程奖励模型(PRM)因训练数据步骤级别不平衡存在隐藏偏差，标准交叉熵训练放大该偏差导致高误报率。提出PRISM框架，采用对比步骤级比较、时间前瞻策略生成难负样本、难度感知课程学习，显著降低误报率并提升PRM在策略优化和搜索任务中的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PRM因步骤级训练数据严重不平衡，标准交叉熵训练会放大偏差，导致PRM过度奖励看似合理但错误的步骤，产生高误报率。这些误报会误导Best-of-N选择、引导解码和策略优化走向错误的推理路径。

Method: 提出PRISM框架：(1)从点式标签拟合转向对比式步骤级比较；(2)采用时间前瞻策略生成难负样本；(3)引入难度感知课程学习优化对比步骤的边际。

Result: 在PRMBench上误报率降低22%，宏F1优于强判别式PRM；在引导解码中准确率提升22%，Best-of-N中提升33%。

Conclusion: 可信的过程监督不仅在于分配高奖励，更在于以正确的原因奖励正确的推理。PRM训练应从点式标签拟合转向可靠的相对比较。

Abstract: Process Reward Models (PRMs) improve credit assignment for reasoning by providing step-level feedback. However, we identify a hidden bias in PRMs caused by severe imbalance in step-level training data. Standard cross-entropy training amplifies this bias, causing PRMs to overcredit plausible but incorrect steps and produce high false-positive rates. We show that these false positives have an asymmetric downstream effect: false negatives mainly slow exploration, whereas false positives actively steer Best-of-N selection, guided decoding, and policy optimization toward flawed reasoning. This suggests that PRM training should shift from pointwise label fitting to reliable relative comparisons. To address this, we propose PRISM (Precision Ranking for Improved Step Modeling), a policy-aware PRM training framework that learns from contrastive step-level comparisons and hard negatives generated by a temporal lookahead strategy, requiring no new human labels. We further use a difficulty-aware curriculum to optimize the contrastive step margin. Across PRMBench and ProcessBench, PRISM substantially reduces false positives (22% on PRMBench) and improves macro F1 over strong discriminative PRMs. When applied to policy optimization and search tasks, including guided decoding and Best-of-N selection, it consistently improves accuracy (up to 22% for guided decoding and 33% for Best-of-N) and robustness. More broadly, trustworthy process supervision is not just about assigning high rewards, but about rewarding the right reasoning for the right reasons.

</details>


### [102] [FlashMemory-DeepSeek-V4: Lightning Index Ultra-Long Context via Lookahead Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09079)
*Yan Wang,Qifan Zhang,Jiachen Yu,Tian Liang,Dongyang Ma,Xiang Hu,Zibo Lin,Chunyang Li,Zhichao Wang,Jia Li,Yujiu Yang,Haitao Mi,Dong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Lookahead Sparse Attention (LSA) 范式，通过神经记忆索引器（基于 DeepSeek-V4 架构的双编码器）主动预测未来上下文需求，仅保留查询关键的 KV 块在 GPU 内存中，可将物理 KV 缓存压缩至全上下文基线的 13.5%，在 500K 极端长度下减少 90% 以上缓存开销，同时保持或略微提升下游准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LLM 在解码时加载完整 KV 缓存，导致超长上下文服务时 GPU 内存严重瓶颈。

Method: 提出 Lookahead Sparse Attention (LSA)，核心是 Neural Memory Indexer（基于 DeepSeek-V4 架构的双编码器），采用无骨干网络解耦训练策略，独立于主干模型训练索引器。

Result: 在 LongBench-v2、LongMemEval、RULER 等评测中，平均物理 KV 缓存压缩至全上下文基线的 13.5%，下游准确率平均提升 0.6%；在 500K 极端长度下缓存开销减少 90% 以上。

Conclusion: "少即是多"的范式显著提升了长上下文服务的效率，同时作为有效的注意力去噪器，保持或提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Conventional LLMs keep the full KV cache loaded during decoding, causing a severe GPU memory bottleneck for ultra-long context serving. In this report, we propose Lookahead Sparse Attention (LSA), a novel inference paradigm powered by a Neural Memory Indexer built upon the DeepSeek-V4 architecture. Rather than passively attending to all historical tokens, LSA proactively predicts future context demands and preserves only the query-critical KV chunks in the GPU memory. Crucially, we instantiate this architecture via a backbone-free decoupled training strategy. By formulating the indexer as a standard dual-encoder architecture, we train it independently using standard retrieval training frameworks without ever loading the massive backbone model into GPU memory.
  We demonstrate that this "less is more" paradigm significantly maximizes serving efficiency while acting as an effective attention denoiser in tasks that rely on long-term global memory. Across primary long-context evaluation suites (e.g., LongBench-v2, LongMemEval, and RULER), FM-DS-V4 compresses the average physical KV cache footprint down to merely 13.5% of the full-context baseline, while consistently preserving or slightly elevating downstream accuracy (+0.6% absolute margin on average). Crucially, at extreme 500K scales, FlashMemory suppresses the physical KV cache overhead by over 90% without destabilizing the backbone's core reasoning capacities.

</details>


### [103] [BrainSurgery: Reproducible and Reliable Declarative Weight Manipulations for Model Editing and Upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09707)
*Gianluca Barmina,Annemette Broch Pirchert,Andrea Blasi Núñez,Lukas Galke Poech,Peter Schneider-Kamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainSurgery 是一个用于神经网络检查点（checkpoint）的"张量手术"工具，通过声明式 YAML 计划支持模型权重的高效管理、检查和修改，涵盖层重构、精度转换、低秩分解等操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型规模增大，管理和修改大型检查点变得越来越困难，现有的 ad-hoc Python 脚本方法脆弱且不可复现。

Method: BrainSurgery 通过抽象存储格式和内存管理，使用声明式 YAML 计划执行复杂的张量变换，支持正则表达式和结构定位的修改、数学变换和张量重塑，并内置断言验证。

Result: 覆盖了从模型升级到 LoRA 提取的四个示例和三个案例研究，展示了工具的有效性。

Conclusion: BrainSurgery 通过可复现和验证的操作，为未来研究提供坚实基础。

Abstract: As deep learning models scale, managing, inspecting, and modifying large checkpoints has become increasingly challenging. Researchers often need to alter model weights for layer restructuring, precision casting, low-rank factorization, and architectural debugging, yet these workflows often rely on fragile ad-hoc Python scripts. Here, we introduce BrainSurgery, a tool for robust and reproducible "tensor surgery" on neural network checkpoints, and provide a system demonstration covering four examples and three case studies from model upcycling to LoRA extraction. By abstracting storage formats and memory management, BrainSurgery executes complex transformations through declarative YAML plans. It supports structural modifications, mathematical transformations, and tensor reshaping through expressive regex and structural targeting, while built-in assertions validate tensor shapes, data types, and values to prevent silent errors. We envision that BrainSurgery will provide a strong foundation for future research through its reproducible and validated operations.

</details>


### [104] [Optimizing Energy-based Neural Network Training with Coherent Ising Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09117)
*Chen-Rui Fan,Bo Lu,Zhi-Hong Zhang,Run-Qing Zhang,Jing-Wei Wen,Chuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用相干伊辛机（CIM）结合平衡传播（Equilibrium Propagation）训练基于能量的神经网络，通过引入Adam优化器提升收敛速度与精度，并展示了其在深层网络和卷积操作上的可扩展性，为下一代AI硬件提供了新型物理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊辛机在硬件连接性和训练方法上存在局限，难以扩展到大规模神经网络，亟需新的训练框架来充分发挥其物理计算潜力。

Method: 使用CIM结合平衡传播训练能量基神经网络，并集成Adam优化器求解Hopfield能量网络的基态，同时在更深层架构和卷积操作上验证可扩展性。

Result: CIM训练性能与现有软件实现相当；Adam优化器显著提升了收敛速度和求解精度；方法在更深网络和卷积操作上可扩展。

Conclusion: CIM动力学可作为训练复杂神经网络的可扩展平台，为通过模拟电路、光电子或集成光子学实现高能效AI硬件提供了新路径。

Abstract: While Ising machines serve as advanced physical solvers for the Ising model,enabling applications in combinatorial optimization and neural network training,their scalability for large-scale neural networks remains constrained by hardware connectivity limitations and suboptimal training methodologies. In this work,we leverage a Coherent Ising Machine (CIM) to train an energy-based neural network using Equilibrium Propagation, achieving performance comparable to existing software-based implementations. We further enhance the algorithm by integrating the Adam optimizer to solve for the ground state of a Hopfield energy network, significantly improving convergence speed and solution accuracy. Additionally, we demonstrate the scalability of our approach across deeper network architectures and convolutional operations. Our results highlight the potential of CIM dynamics as a scalable platform for training complex neural networks, offering a pathway toward energy-efficient implementations via analog circuits, optoelectronics, or integrated photonics. This work establishes a novel physical framework for next-generation AI hardware development.

</details>


### [105] [Toward Compiler World Models: Learning Latent Dynamics for Efficient Tensor Program Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09312)
*Haolin Pan,Lianghong Huang,Xvlin Zhou,Mingjie Xing,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受世界模型启发的评估器，将调度评估建模为基于动作条件的程序状态潜在动力学过程，在TVM AutoScheduler中实现，相比Ansor在64次试验预算下GPU加速1.37倍、CPU加速1.54倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动调度器使用学习到的成本模型来减少评估开销，但通常将每个候选视为静态代码快照，忽略了产生它的调度轨迹，导致对动作依赖不敏感且易受表面代码变化影响。

Method: 将调度评估建模为动作条件化的潜在动力学过程：从初始程序开始，在连续潜在空间中用轻量级过渡模型展开调度动作，避免昂贵的AST突变和重复编码，最终将动态表示与动作和硬件特征结合来排序候选方案。

Result: 在TVM AutoScheduler中实现，在64次试验预算下，GPU上比Ansor提升1.37倍，CPU上提升1.54倍；在十分之一测量次数下匹配Ansor-10K的几何平均误差在2.2%内；全模型推理相比PyTorch/PyTorch-opt(cuDNN)加速4.61倍/3.67倍。

Conclusion: 提出的世界模型启发方法能有效捕捉调度动作依赖关系，显著提升自动调度器的搜索效率和最终性能。

Abstract: Tensor program optimization is essential for modern machine learning systems, but its search space is enormous. Existing auto-schedulers reduce measurement cost with learned cost models, yet they usually evaluate each candidate as a static code snapshot, ignoring the schedule trajectory that produced it. This makes them insensitive to action dependencies and vulnerable to superficial code variations. We propose a \emph{world-model-inspired} evaluator that models schedule evaluation as action-conditioned latent dynamics over program states. Starting from the initial program, it rolls out scheduling actions in a continuous latent space with a lightweight transition model, avoiding expensive AST mutation and repeated code encoding. The final dynamic representation is combined with action and hardware features to rank candidates. Implemented in TVM AutoScheduler, our method improves representative-subgraph latency over Ansor by 1.37$\times$ on GPU and 1.54$\times$ on CPU under the same 64-trial budget. It also matches Ansor-10K within 2.2% geometric mean using 10$\times$ fewer measurements, and accelerates full-model inference over PyTorch/PyTorch-opt(cuDNN) by 4.61$\times$/3.67$\times$ geometric mean.

</details>


### [106] [Scaling Neural Network Verification with Tensor Parallelism and Fully Sharded Data Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09377)
*Sergei Vorobyov,Eugene Ilyushin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对形式化神经网络验证中的GPU内存瓶颈，将张量并行（TP）和全分片数据并行（FSDP）两种并行技术适配到auto_LiRPA/α,β-CROWN验证框架中。TP将权重和A矩阵跨GPU分片，在P=2时实现约2倍峰值内存降低但边界紧度下降；FSDP仅分片权重矩阵并使用逐层AllGather，产生与单GPU基线比特一致的边界，基线内存降低80-90%，峰值内存降低34-39%，并能干净地集成到完整验证和卷积层中。实验发现α-CROWN+BaB模式下的内存瓶颈是每神经元alpha张量而非权重矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化神经网络验证（证明网络在指定输入域内所有输入上满足安全属性）在实践中受限于GPU内存：标准边界传播算法（IBP, CROWN, α-CROWN）的实现要求权重和松弛系数矩阵完全驻留在单个加速器上，严重限制了可验证网络的规模。

Method: 将大规模模型训练中发展的两种并行技术——张量并行（TP）和全分片数据并行（FSDP）——适配到auto_LiRPA/α,β-CROWN验证框架。TP同时分片权重和A矩阵；FSDP仅分片权重矩阵，每层使用AllGather操作恢复完整权重后进行前向传播。

Result: TP在P=2时实现约2倍峰值内存降低，但边界紧度因分片区域内部被迫替换为IBP而下降；FSDP产生与单GPU基线比特一致的边界，基线内存降低80-90%，峰值内存降低34-39%。FSDP可集成到完整验证（β-CROWN+Branch-and-Bound）和卷积层（BoundConv），在CIFAR-100 ResNet-large上获得完整unsat结果。实验揭示α-CROWN+BaB模式下的真正瓶颈是每神经元alpha张量而非权重矩阵。

Conclusion: FSDP是当前更优的并行策略，能在保持边界精度的同时显著降低内存消耗，并支持完整验证流程。未来方向应聚焦于解决每神经元alpha张量的内存瓶颈，而非权重矩阵。

Abstract: Formal neural network verification -- proving that a network satisfies safety properties for \emph{all} inputs in a specified domain -- is bounded in practice by GPU memory: standard implementations of bound-propagation algorithms (IBP, CROWN, $α$-CROWN) require weight and relaxation-coefficient matrices to reside entirely on one accelerator. We adapt two parallelism techniques originally developed for large-scale model training to the \texttt{auto\_LiRPA}\,/\,$α,β$-CROWN verification framework. \textbf{Tensor Parallelism (TP)} shards both weight and $A$-matrices across GPUs, achieving ${\approx}2\times$ peak-memory reduction at $P{=}2$; soundness is confirmed on VNN-COMP 2022 MNIST-FC benchmarks, though bound tightness degrades with the number of sharded zones due to forced IBP substitution for intermediate bounds inside sharded zones. \textbf{Fully Sharded Data Parallelism (FSDP)} shards only weight matrices with a per-layer \texttt{AllGather}, producing bounds that are \emph{bitwise identical} to the single-GPU baseline: baseline memory drops by 80--90\%, peak memory by 34--39\% on wide MLPs. FSDP integrates cleanly with complete verification ($β$-CROWN + Branch-and-Bound) and with convolutional layers (\texttt{BoundConv}); a complete \emph{unsat} result is obtained for CIFAR-100 ResNet-large (VNN-COMP 2024) under FSDP. Across all experiments the memory bottleneck in $α$-CROWN+BaB mode proves to be per-neuron alpha tensors, not weight matrices, pointing to the key direction for future work.

</details>


### [107] [Distilling Safe LLM Systems via Soft Prompts for On Device Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09388)
*Motasem Alfarra,Cristina Pinneri,Dana Kianfar,Mohammed Almousa,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在资源受限的边缘设备上部署安全大语言模型（LLM）的参数高效安全对齐方法。通过系统评估，发现软提示结合基于总变差和KL散度的蒸馏训练在安全-效用权衡上优于LoRA适配器、引导向量和直接优化方法，且推理时仅需极少的额外内存与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上部署安全的LLM面临重大挑战：传统的双模型系统（LLM+防护模型）虽然提供有效的安全保障，但其巨大的内存和计算需求使其难以在设备端部署。需要更高效的参数级安全对齐方法。

Method: 系统评估多种LLM架构、训练目标和参数高效微调方法，引入基于总变差和KL散度的蒸馏框架，将防护模型的安全行为迁移到可学习的软提示中。

Result: 软提示结合蒸馏训练在安全-效用权衡上优于LoRA适配器、引导向量和直接优化方法，且推理时内存和计算开销极低。

Conclusion: 软提示蒸馏是在设备端LLM部署中进行安全对齐的首选方法。

Abstract: Deploying safe large language models (LLMs) on resource-constrained edge devices presents a critical challenge: while dual-model systems combining LLMs with guard models provide effective safety guarantees, their substantial memory and computational demands make them prohibitively expensive for on-device deployment. This paper presents a comprehensive study of parameter-efficient safety alignment methods for resource-constrained settings. Through systematic evaluation across multiple LLM architectures, training objectives, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning approaches, we identify that soft prompts combined with distillation-based training consistently outperform alternative methods. We introduce distillation frameworks based on total variation and KL divergence that effectively transfer safety behaviors from guard models into learned soft prompts. Our evaluations on various benchmarks demonstrate that this combination achieves superior safety-usefulness trade-offs compared to LoRA adapters, steering vectors, and direct optimization methods, while requiring minimal additional memory and compute at inference time. These findings establish soft prompt distillation as the preferred approach for safety alignment in on-device LLM deployment.

</details>


### [108] [BUDDY: BUdget-Driven DYnamic Depth Routing for Adaptive Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09514)
*Yuhua Zhou,Shaoqi Yu,Shichao Weng,Changhai Zhou,Mingze Yin,Fei Yang,Aimin Pan*

Main category: cs.LG
